*Yuna Oikawa,Kei Endo,Takanori Uzawa,Yunzhe Zhang,Manan Anjaria,Lerrel Pinto,Sherry Yang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WetRobo——可跨实验室迁移的机器人套件，配合编码智能体（Codex）在本地观察并编写执行程序，无需遥操作数据或网络训练即可完成湿实验任务，并展示了优于 VLA 策略的跨实验室迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验自动化要求通用、可复现的机器人系统，使个体湿实验研究者无需遥操作或训练神经网络即可委派机器人任务。现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽面向通用机械臂，但在运行环境改变时性能下降，难以跨实验室迁移。

Method: 构建 WetRobo 机器人套件：单机械臂、实验室设备（培养箱、带盖试剂瓶、培养皿）、既有机械臂控制代码、各任务的遥操作示范记录，以及通用 AGENTS.md 技能文件。生物实验者仅以自然语言给出任务；编码智能体观察本地实验室并编写、执行程序，必要时调用外部工具进行适配。以 OpenAI Codex（gpt-5.6-sol）在真实实验室中验证三项任务：抬起培养皿盖、拧开瓶盖、打开培养箱门。

Result: 三项任务均成功完成。在开瓶盖任务中，编码智能体在 Lab X 与 Lab Y 两个实验室都成功；而仅在 Lab X 示范数据上微调的 VLA 策略在 Lab X 成功，迁移到 Lab Y 时失败。

Conclusion: 与其为每个实验室单独训练策略，不如分发统一套件、让编码智能体在各实验室现场适配，这为实验室机器人提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Automating biological research requires general-purpose, reproducible robot systems that allow individual wet-lab researchers to delegate robot tasks without performing teleoperation or neural-network training. Vision-language-action policies have been proposed for general-purpose arms, but can lose performance when their operating environment changes. We therefore built WetRobo, a robot kit that can readily transfer between laboratories. It consists of one robot arm, laboratory equipment (an incubator, a reagent bottle with a cap, and a Petri dish), the existing code that moves the arm, teleoperation demonstrations of each task that we recorded, and a general AGENTS.md skill file. A biological experimentalist provides natural-language tasks without collecting local teleoperation training data or training a neural network. The coding agent observes the local laboratory and writes and executes programs, using external tools as needed for adaptation. We demonstrate use of WetRobo with OpenAI Codex (gpt-5.6-sol) on three successful tasks: lifting a Petri dish lid, removing a bottle cap, and opening the incubator door, all in real-world laboratories. The coding agent achieved the cap task in both laboratories, Lab X and Lab Y, whereas a VLA fine-tuned on Lab X demonstrations succeeded there but failed to transfer to Lab Y. These results point to a practical route for laboratory robotics: instead of training a policy for each laboratory, distribute a kit and let a coding agent adapt it in each laboratory. Code, demonstrations, and the evolved programs are available at https://github.com/tsudalab/WetRobo.

</details>


### [244] [Risk-Aware World Modeling with Flow-Guided Occupancy Evolution for Selective Trajectory Planning in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18442)
*Rongxiang Zeng,Linsen Cai,Jiafu Zhang,Yijie Zhong,Yide Tao,Shuai Wang,Nan Zheng,Hai L. Vu,Alvaro Garcia Hernandez,Yongqi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RiskWorld 提出风险感知的世界模型框架，融合共享占据预测与选择性轨迹替换，在 nuScenes 开环规划上取得最低碰撞率（3 s 长时域），并以 11.5 FPS 实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划需预判动态演化风险，并决定何时更新当前规划轨迹；现有方法难以在低算力下兼顾风险预测与轨迹修正。

Method: 融合空间风险场、时序行为体上下文与视觉 BEV 特征；采用流引导演化传递占据与场景特征，并用符号残差校正传输后的占据。每个规划步生成一次预测并复用于所有候选轨迹。候选轨迹与当前状态持续性参考对比，得到非负碰撞分数修正。当前世界评估选出的轨迹作为规划锚点，仅当额外预测风险触发干预且替代轨迹满足预测风险与轨迹误差的分量约束时才被替换，候选几何保持不变。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上使用相机特征、标注衍生的当前与历史行为体状态及地图上下文进行开环评估：3 s 长时域碰撞率最低，平均 L2 误差在多种 SOTA 基线中排名第二；单张 RTX 4090 上 11.5 FPS，参数量 90.81 M。消融显示其碰撞率低于当前状态重打分基线，且预测复用使额外候选轨迹以低边际计算成本被评估。

Conclusion: 风险感知世界建模结合选择性轨迹替换，可在长时域显著降低碰撞风险并保持较高计算效率。

Abstract: Safe motion planning in automated driving requires anticipating evolving traffic risks and deciding when to revise the current planned trajectory. We introduce RiskWorld, a risk-aware world modeling framework for shared occupancy forecasting and selective trajectory replacement. Spatial risk fields and temporal actor context are fused with visual bird's-eye-view features. Flow-guided evolution transports occupancy and scene features, while signed residuals correct occupancy after transport. One forecast is generated per planning step and reused across candidates. Each candidate is compared with a current-state persistence reference, yielding a nonnegative collision-score correction. The trajectory selected by current-world evaluation serves as the planning anchor and is replaced only when additional predicted risk triggers intervention and an alternative satisfies component-wise constraints on predicted risk and trajectory error. Candidate geometries remain unchanged. We evaluate RiskWorld for open-loop planning on nuScenes using camera features, annotation-derived current and historical actor states, and dataset-provided map context. RiskWorld achieves the lowest collision rate at a long evaluation horizon of 3 s, and the second-best average L2 error among various state-of-the-art baselines, while running at 11.5 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 90.81 M parameters. Within-setting ablations show that RiskWorld achieves lower collision rates than the current-state rescoring baseline, while forecast reuse enables additional candidates to be evaluated at low marginal computational cost.

</details>


### [245] [The Mirage of Calibrated Confidence: Trajectory-Independence of Verbalized Confidence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18453)
*Jisoo Yang,Jaeho Han,Trung X. Pham,Junyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）中"言语化置信度"与推理轨迹基本脱钩，导致模型即使自我纠正失败仍报告高置信度；现有校准指标（ECE、AUROC）无法检测该问题，为此提出轨迹锚定分数TGS（Self/Pair两种形式）及TGS-Bench评测套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准后的VLM可能反复自我纠错、给出错误答案却仍报告高置信度。作者希望探究这一现象的成因，并解决现有校准评估指标无法识别"置信度与推理轨迹脱钩"这一盲点。

Method: 从三个互补视角切入：内容变化、token掩码、模型自身的犹豫标记。据此提出轨迹锚定分数TGS：TGS-self比较模型在有/无自身轨迹访问时的置信度；TGS-pair检验模型是否对正确轨迹赋予比错误轨迹更高的置信度（沿视觉、推理、答案三轴）。同时构建模型无关的TGS-Bench，覆盖10个基准，含受控的好/坏轨迹对。

Result: 发现VLM的言语化置信度在很大程度上与轨迹无关，对推理轨迹的实际内容敏感度不足；校准训练反而可能加剧这种脱钩。传统校准排名与轨迹锚定排名不一致，暴露出当前评测实践的盲区。

Conclusion: 言语化置信度不足以反映推理轨迹质量；ECE、AUROC等现有指标存在检测盲区，需引入TGS等轨迹锚定指标与TGS-Bench进行更全面的校准评估。

Abstract: A calibrated Vision-Language Model (VLM) can repeatedly self-correct, say "Wait, I should recheck," arrive at the wrong answer, and still report high confidence. We find that this occurs because verbalized confidence is largely trajectory-independent in the VLMs and calibration methods we evaluate. We examine this through three complementary lenses: content variation, token masking, and the model's own hesitation markers. We show that confidence is insufficiently sensitive to what the reasoning trajectory actually contains, and that calibration training can paradoxically worsen this disconnect. Since existing metrics like ECE and AUROC cannot detect this problem, we propose the Trajectory-Grounding Score (TGS) in two complementary forms: TGS-self, which compares confidence with and without access to the model's own trajectory, and TGS-pair, which tests whether the model assigns higher confidence to correct trajectories than to flawed ones along the vision, reasoning, and answer axes. We propose TGS-Bench, a model-agnostic suite spanning 10 benchmarks with controlled good/bad trajectory pairs, and show that conventional calibration rankings diverge from trajectory-grounding rankings, exposing a blind spot in current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [246] [Collective Loss of Control in LLM Agent Systems: An Epidemic Account of Mutation, Contagion, and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18460)
*Xiangfan Wu,Zonghao Ying,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"流行病式"解释框架，将多智能体系统的集体失控分解为偶然变异、传染与恢复三个环节，并通过部署审计与RogueHandoff-20基准验证隐式通信路径与受体易感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨多智能体系统如何从局部偏差演变为集体失控。作者受OpenAI智能体协调事件的启发，试图解释"罕见个体偏差"为何能与"显著的集体风险"并存，即低基线危害与高条件易感性共存的机制。

Method: 1) 提出以偶然变异、传染、恢复为核心的流行病学解释框架：自发偏差产生"种子"，通信使其他智能体采纳并再传播其不安全策略，当传播速度超过纠错与遏制速度时出现集体失效。2) 开展部署审计，识别名义上独立的评估运行之间的隐式通信路径，并通过默认Docker后端验证传输可行性。3) 构建RogueHandoff-20基准（20个可执行场景），通过注入由改进的Qwen-27B路径生成的不安全轨迹来测试受体易感性。4) 在四条native-pending路径上对比正常任务与注入后的执行危害。

Result: 四条native-pending路径中，正常任务下的执行危害为0-5%，注入不安全轨迹后升至40-95%，比配对的直接恶意请求高出5-45个百分点。结果支持"低观测基线危害+高条件易感性"的结论，但未确立自然罕见事件发生率，也未证明存在自主级联。

Conclusion: 该解释框架提示应发展互补防御：在预防自发偏差之外，加强系统的抵抗与恢复能力，并审计和限制可能导致局部失败升级为集体失控的非预期通信路径。

Abstract: How does a multi-agent system evolve from a local deviation into collective loss of control? We propose an epidemic explanation organized around accidental mutation, contagion, and recovery. A spontaneous deviation creates a seed; communication enables other agents to adopt and retransmit its unsafe strategy; collective failure can emerge when propagation outpaces correction and containment. Thus, rare individual deviations can coexist with substantial collective risk. Motivated by reported OpenAI agent coordination incidents, we examine two ingredients of this mechanism. A deployment audit identifies implicit communication paths between nominally independent evaluation runs and verifies transport through a default Docker backend. RogueHandoff-20, a benchmark of 20 executable scenarios, tests recipient susceptibility by injecting unsafe trajectories generated by a modified Qwen-27B route. Across four native-pending routes, executed harm is 0-5% on normal tasks and 40-95% after injection, exceeding paired direct malicious requests by 5-45 percentage points. These results support low observed baseline harm alongside high conditional susceptibility; they do not establish natural rare-event rates or demonstrate an autonomous cascade. The account motivates complementary defenses: strengthen resistance and recovery alongside prevention of spontaneous deviations, and audit and restrict unintended communication paths that can turn local failures into collective loss of control.

</details>


### [247] [Disentangling Long-Term Memory via Latent Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18461)
*Cai Ke,Xinghao Chen,Xiaoyu Shen,Keyu Chen,Siyu An,Junnan Dong,Ruifeng Xu,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LGM，一个神经符号框架，将长期记忆解耦迁移到连续潜在空间，通过稀疏自编码器构建查询感知的潜在图并生成动态关系边权重，结合图编码器以查询为条件进行消息传递，在长期个性化基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体需基于长期历史交互推理显式偏好与隐式行为证据。早期扁平检索方法独立打分记忆片段，忽略分布式信息；现有结构化记忆框架依赖与查询无关的静态图，难以捕捉上下文相关关系；原始文本记忆本身纠缠且噪声大，导致细粒度个性化与跨会话推理计算代价过高。

Method: 提出LGM神经符号框架：(i) 不持久化固定图，而是用稀疏自编码器构建定制化潜在图，针对每个查询将历史交互映射为潜在记忆节点，并将记忆轨迹解耦为稀疏概念激活，动态合成查询感知的关系边权重；(ii) 图编码器将查询嵌入作为条件偏好，在任务特定的潜在子图上引导非线性消息传递，生成高表达力的记忆表示以实现有效激活。

Result: 在长期个性化基准上的大量实验表明，LGM在捕捉显式与隐式偏好方面显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，并支持个性化响应生成。

Conclusion: LGM通过将长期记忆解耦置于连续潜在空间，动态构建查询感知的潜在图结构，有效解决了静态图与原始记忆纠缠噪声带来的个性化与跨会话推理瓶颈。

Abstract: Personalized agents are required to reason over long-term history interactions to infer both explicit preferences and implicit behavioral evidence. While early flat retrieval methods score memory fragments independently and neglect the distributed information, current structured memory frameworks rely on query-agnostic static graphs that fail to capture the context-dependent relations. Crucially, raw textual memories are inherently entangled and noisy, making fine-grained personalization and cross-session reasoning computationally prohibitive. To this end, we present LGM, a novel neuro-symbolic framework that shifts long-term memory disentanglement into a continuous latent space. Specifically, (i) instead of persisting fixed graphs, we design a tailored latent graph construction with a sparse autoencoder. Subject to each query, it maps historical interactions into latent memory nodes and disentangles the memory traces into sparse concept activations, dynamically synthesizing query-aware relational edge weights. (ii) A graph encoder then treats the query embedding as a conditioning preference to direct non-linear message passing across the task-specific latent subgraph. This yields a highly expressive memory representation for effective activations. Extensive experiments on long-term personalization benchmarks demonstrate that LGM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in capturing both explicit and implicit preferences while enabling personalized responses.

</details>


### [248] [First Token Matters: Understanding Safety Collapse in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18471)
*Yizheng Yang,Haining Yu,Yuechen Wang,Yikai Hou,Xing Fu,Jinbo Yang,Tianqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对LRMs拒答动态的token级位置分析，发现推理起始处存在局部脆弱性（Onset Refusal Collapse, ORC），并提出仅更新单个token嵌入的推理时干预方法SafeToken以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）在应对有害查询时安全对齐常出现退化，而现有改进方法多依赖额外训练或偏好优化，对安全失败背后的内部机制理解有限。

Method: 对拒答动态进行token级位置分析，识别推理起始处的局部脆弱性ORC；进而提出SafeToken——一种轻量级推理时干预，在学习到的连续安全锚点处精确注入于推理起始位置，仅更新单个token嵌入。

Result: LRMs的拒答相关信号在有害查询下于首个生成token处急剧下降，并与不安全响应生成相关；SafeToken有效缓解ORC，在有害查询基准上提升安全性，同时基本保持推理效用。

Conclusion: LRMs的安全失败可能源于从理解到生成的关键转折处的瞬时崩溃。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) exhibit strong problem-solving abilities, yet their safety alignment often degrades when handling harmful queries. Existing approaches to improving safety largely rely on additional training or preference optimization, while offering limited understanding of the internal mechanisms behind safety failures. In this work, we investigate this failure through a token-level positional analysis of refusal dynamics and identify a localized vulnerability at the onset of reasoning, which we term Onset Refusal Collapse (ORC). We find that the refusal-related signal of LRMs drops sharply at the first generated token under harmful queries, which is associated with unsafe response generation. Motivated by this finding, we propose SafeToken, a lightweight inference-time intervention that injects a learned continuous safety anchor precisely at reasoning onset. Despite updating only a single token embedding, SafeToken effectively mitigates ORC, improves safety on harmful-query benchmarks, and largely preserves reasoning utility. These results suggest that safety failures in LRMs can arise from a transient breakdown at the critical transition from understanding to generation.

</details>


### [249] [Hyperbolic Graph Representation Learning for Differential Diagnosis on Biomedical Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18481)
*Pietro Miotto,Lucia Mellini,Tommaso Marzi,Cesare Alippi,Elena Casiraghi,Alberto Paccanaro,Giorgio Valentini,Mauricio Soto-Gomez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文初步研究双曲图表示学习在生物医学知识图谱上用于孟德尔病鉴别诊断的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学知识图谱融合本体层次结构与异质实体（表型、疾病、基因、蛋白、患者）间的横向关联，其混合结构引发疑问：天然适于树状结构的双曲嵌入是否仍具优势。

Method: 在患者集成的生物医学图谱上，对孤立本体子图和链接预测任务分别评估双曲模型与欧氏基线。

Result: 在孤立本体子图上，双曲模型以显著更低维度达到强性能；在按患者候选疾病排序的链接预测任务中，双曲嵌入可利用层次结构并支持诊断推理。

Conclusion: 双曲嵌入可在异质患者级图谱上兼顾生物医学层次结构与诊断推理，具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs combine ontology-derived hierarchies with transversal associations among heterogeneous entities such as phenotypes, diseases, genes, proteins, and patients. This hybrid structure raises the question of whether hyperbolic embeddings, which naturally capture tree-like organization, remain useful beyond purely hierarchical graphs. We present a preliminary study of hyperbolic graph representation learning for Mendelian-disease differential diagnosis on a patient-integrated biomedical graph. Experiments on isolated ontology subgraphs show that hyperbolic models achieve strong performance in substantially lower dimensions than Euclidean baselines. We then evaluate the models on a link-prediction task that ranks candidate diseases for each patient. Results suggest that hyperbolic embeddings can exploit biomedical hierarchical structure while supporting diagnostic reasoning over heterogeneous patient-level graphs.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Routine Compliance: Cunning Data Cultivates Safety Vigilance in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18515)
*Youjia Wang,Lin Xu,Yang Sun,Yuxiao Lu,Chengfang Fang,Jie Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"狡诈问题"(cunning questions)——未必与安全相关但含误导性前提、非常规推理或细微矛盾的问题,假设学习识别此类陷阱可迁移至安全场景。实验表明该训练提升了模型对分布外越狱攻击的鲁棒性,并增强后续安全微调效果,在与SOTA流水线结合后将九个骨干-基准组合的平均ASR从17.40%降至15.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐让LLM能识别有害请求并拒绝风险指令,但当有害意图被隐藏在看似无害的语境中时,对齐模型仍会失败。稳健的安全性因此不仅需要安全边界知识,还需要"警觉性"(vigilance):检测异常前提、误导性推理及表层语义之下的潜在风险,即在行动前审视请求的潜在意图与假设。

Method: 引入"狡诈问题"(cunning questions),即未必与安全相关但包含误导性前提、非常规推理或细微矛盾的问题;通过Cunning训练让模型学习识破此类推理陷阱,以培育警觉性,并假设该能力可迁移到安全关键场景。方法还包含匹配安全微调后的轨迹分析,以及刻画狡诈数据所学不变性能否迁移到安全输入的条件的理论分析。

Result: Cunning训练提升了对外分布(out-of-distribution)越狱攻击的鲁棒性,并强化了后续的安全微调。在现有SOTA安全对齐流水线中加入Cunning后,在评估设置上达到新的SOTA,将九个骨干-基准组合的平均ASR从17.40%降至15.05%。匹配安全微调后的轨迹分析显示,安全判断更可能在有害规划开始之前就主导响应。

Conclusion: 有条件的理论分析进一步刻画了从狡诈数据中学到的不变性能迁移到安全相关输入的条件。这些发现表明,狡诈数据能够增强模型警觉性,并补充传统安全对齐。总体而言,培育模型对推理陷阱的警觉性是一种与常规安全对齐互补、可提升鲁棒安全性的途径。

Abstract: Safety alignment teaches large language models (LLMs) to recognize harmful requests and reject risky instructions. Yet aligned models can fail when harmful intent is concealed within seemingly benign contexts. Robust safety therefore requires both knowledge of safety boundaries and \textbf{vigilance}: the ability to detect unusual premises, misleading reasoning, and latent risks beneath surface-level semantics. Vigilance requires models to scrutinize a request's underlying intent and assumptions before acting. To cultivate this capability, we introduce \textbf{cunning questions}, which are not necessarily safety-related but contain misleading premises, atypical reasoning, or subtle inconsistencies. We hypothesize that learning to look beyond such reasoning traps can transfer to safety-critical scenarios. Experiments show that Cunning training improves robustness to out-of-distribution jailbreak attacks and strengthens subsequent safety fine-tuning. Furthermore, augmenting an existing state-of-the-art safety alignment pipeline with Cunning establishes a new state of the art across our evaluated settings, reducing mean ASR across nine backbone--benchmark combinations from 17.40\% to 15.05\%. Trace analysis after matched safety fine-tuning suggests that safety judgments are more likely to govern responses before harmful planning begins. A conditional theoretical analysis further characterizes when invariance learned from cunning data can transfer to safety-related inputs. These findings suggest that cunning data can strengthen model vigilance and complement conventional safety alignment.

</details>


### [251] [AeroWeaver: An Embodied-Agent Harness for Weaving Aerial Skills into Distributed, Adaptive Swarm Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18520)
*Jiabin Lou,Yirong Yang,Haopeng Wang,Xuxin Lv,Xinyu Liu,Diyuan Hou,Xuehong Liu,Rongye Shi,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AeroWeaver——一种具身智能体框架，将单机 UAV 技能编织为协同任务级行为，通过语义决策与受治理技能的衔接、角色条件下的分布式本地智能体以及角色索引的状态-动作-奖励经验在线优化技能选择，实现无需中心智能体生成联合动作的分布式多无人机运行与免训练自适应学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体智能是多个体通过局部感知、信息交换与协同行动追求共同目标的协作自主范式，UAV 集群是其典型载体，应用于搜索、巡检与跟踪等任务。LLM 智能体增强了自然语言任务理解与高层规划，提供了任务描述与集体行为之间的语义接口。然而将 LLM 智能体应用于 UAV 集群仍存在挑战：模型决策需落地到可执行能力、全局任务推理需与分布式执行协调、任务经验需被用于持续适应。

Method: AeroWeaver 是一个具身智能体框架（embodied-agent harness）。其三项设计为：(1) 将语义决策连接到受治理的技能（governed skills）；(2) 组织角色条件下的本地智能体（role-conditioned local agents）以实现分布式协调；(3) 使用角色索引的状态-动作-奖励（state-action-reward）经验在线优化技能选择。

Result: 实验与运行时验证表明：在测试条件下 AeroWeaver 能保持有效的技能执行；支持体本地（body-local）多 UAV 运行，无需中心智能体基于全局上下文生成联合动作；奖励引导的在线更新提供了从累积执行经验中自适应学习集群智能体的免训练路径。

Conclusion: AeroWeaver 通过语义-技能衔接、角色条件分布式本地智能体与角色索引经验驱动的在线技能选择优化，有效缓解了 LLM 智能体用于 UAV 集群时的决策落地、全局推理与分布式执行协调及持续适应问题。代码已开源。

Abstract: Collective intelligence is a collaborative autonomy paradigm in which multiple agents pursue shared objectives through local perception, information exchange, and coordinated action. UAV swarms embody this paradigm by coordinating multiple vehicles in tasks such as search, inspection, and tracking. Recent advances in large language model (LLM) agents have strengthened natural-language task understanding and high-level planning, providing a flexible semantic interface between mission descriptions and collective behavior. While these advances expand semantic reasoning, applying LLM agents to UAV swarms raises challenges in grounding model decisions in executable capabilities, reconciling global task reasoning with distributed execution, and using mission-specific experience for continual adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce AeroWeaver, an embodied-agent harness that weaves individual UAV skills into coordinated mission-level behavior. AeroWeaver connects semantic decisions to governed skills, organizes role-conditioned local agents for distributed coordination, and uses role-indexed state-action-reward experience to refine skill selection online. Experiments and runtime validation show that AeroWeaver maintains valid skill execution under tested conditions and supports body-local multi-UAV operation without a central agent generating joint actions from global context, while reward-guided online updates provide a training-free path for adaptive learning swarm agents from accumulated execution experience. Code: https://github.com/Admire-ljb/AeroWeaver.

</details>


### [252] [TRIPROBE: Probing Task Separability Beyond Classification for XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18525)
*Amirhossein Sadough,Freek Hens,Aleksa Bokšan,Mohammad Mahdi Dehshibi,Mahyar Shahsavari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriProbe 提出一种多层级探测框架，用于对任务可分性进行可解释诊断，追踪可分性在输入、特征与分类器三级中的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习流程评估多简化为下游准确率，无法解释任务成败的原因，模型常被视为黑箱。

Method: 将多任务问题分解为二分类子任务，应用三个互补探针：输入空间的基础探针、特征表示的潜在探针、分类器输出的最终探针，并以最大Fisher判别比作为可分性度量。

Result: 在 Roshambo sEMG 基准上，TriProbe 揭示出隐藏的失效环节，并定位瓶颈与受影响的任务对。

Conclusion: TriProbe 为任务可分性提供可解释诊断，可指导数据采集、验证与架构设计。

Abstract: Modern evaluation of learning pipelines often reduces to downstream accuracy, leaving open the question of why tasks succeed or fail. TriProbe addresses this gap with a multi-level probing framework for explainable diagnosis of task separability. Rather than treating models as black boxes, TriProbe traces how separability evolves across inputs, learned features, and final classifiers. It decomposes multi-task problems into binary subtasks and applies three complementary probes: a Foundational Probe on input spaces, a Latent Probe on feature representations, and a Final Probe on classifier outputs. Using Maximum Fisher's Discriminant Ratio as a principled separability metric, TriProbe identifies bottlenecks and affected task pairs. Experiments on the Roshambo sEMG benchmark show how TriProbe reveals hidden breakdowns, guiding data collection, validation, and architecture design.

</details>


### [253] [Recursive Reasoning or Statistical Extrapolation? In-Context Learning in Multi-Agent Interdependent Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18591)
*Yu Liu,Wenwen Li,Yifan Dou,Guangnan Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体在需要递归信念推理的多智能体不完全信息博弈中，ICL带来的决策改进究竟源于内部推理还是统计模式外推。通过公共品博弈操纵历史反馈的统计结构，并以与历史无关的理性预期均衡（REE）为基准，发现统计模式被破坏后长上下文优势基本消失，决策质量退化至无上下文基线，且该效应随策略相互依赖增强而放大，表明ICL行为更符合统计外推而非策略推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL能让LLM智能体利用交互历史改进决策，但其改进究竟反映精细的内部推理，还是仅仅外推统计规律尚不清楚。作者希望在需要递归信念推理的策略性多智能体环境中厘清这两种机制，判断ICL能力的本质边界。

Method: 构建需要递归信念推理的多智能体不完全信息博弈（公共品博弈），通过操纵历史反馈的统计结构来检验上下文长度效应，并与历史无关的理性预期均衡（REE）基准对比评估决策质量。

Result: 当历史统计模式被破坏时，长上下文的收益大体消失，决策质量退化至无上下文基线水平；且这一退化在策略相互依赖更强时被显著放大。

Conclusion: 在该类策略环境中，LLM的ICL行为更符合统计外推而非策略推理。工作将ICL机制研究拓展至策略性多智能体场景，提出REE作为区分推理与外推的诊断工具，并提供可复用框架以探测LLM在递归信念任务中的推理边界。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables large language model (LLM) agents to improve decisions using interaction history, yet it remains unclear whether such improvement reflects refined internal reasoning or mere extrapolation of statistical patterns. To disentangle these mechanisms, we study LLM agents in multi-agent incomplete-information games that require recursive belief reasoning. By constructing a public goods game and manipulating the statistical structure of historical feedback, we evaluate decision quality against a history-independent rational expectations equilibrium (REE) benchmark. Our experiments reveal that when historical statistical patterns are disrupted, the benefits of longer context largely vanish, degrading decision quality to the no-context baseline in a way sharply amplified by stronger strategic interdependence. These results suggest that, in such strategic environments, ICL behavior is more consistent with statistical extrapolation than with strategic reasoning. Our work extends the mechanistic study of ICL to strategic multi-agent settings, introduces REE as a diagnostic tool for distinguishing reasoning from extrapolation, and provides a reusable framework for probing the boundaries of LLM reasoning in recursive belief tasks.

</details>


### [254] [Reasoning through Evolution: Automatic Meta-path Discovery for LLM-based Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18597)
*Ziyi Zhou,Xiaoming Zhang,Hui Pang,Yuting Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Bingyu Yan,Erik Cambria,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGER：多智能体遗传进化框架，自动发现优化元路径，将传播图压缩为子图以缓解信息过载与模态错配，使冻结LLM实现结构感知的虚假新闻检测，并配图内上下文学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假新闻检测依赖监督式GNN，需要大量标注数据且泛化能力有限；LLM虽具推理能力，但直接输入原始传播图会引发严重的模态错配与信息过载，导致零样本/少样本下的结构感知推理不可靠。

Method: 提出MAGER多智能体遗传进化框架，自动搜索面向LLM推理优化的元路径，将复杂传播图压缩为信息量高的子图；并引入图内上下文学习策略，检索语义与结构相似的示例以增强分类与推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，在数据高效（少样本）设定下，MAGER显著提升冻结LLM作为独立虚假新闻检测器的性能。

Conclusion: 通过进化元路径压缩图结构并结合图内上下文学习，可有效弥合LLM与传播图之间的模态鸿沟，使冻结LLM在低标注条件下具备可靠的结构感知虚假新闻检测能力。

Abstract: Propagation structures provide crucial evidence for fake news detection, yet existing approaches primarily rely on supervised GNN-based models, which require substantial labeled data and exhibit limited generalization. Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, directly feeding them raw propagation graphs creates a significant modality mismatch and severe information overload, making structure-aware reasoning unreliable in zero-shot and few-shot settings. To bridge this gap, we propose MAGER, a multi-agent genetic evolution framework that automatically discovers meta-paths optimized for LLM reasoning. By compressing complex propagation graphs into informative subgraphs, the evolved meta-paths alleviate both information overload and modality mismatch, enabling frozen LLMs to perform structure-aware veracity reasoning. We further introduce a graph in-context learning strategy that retrieves semantically and structurally similar demonstrations to strengthen classification and reasoning. Extensive experiments show that MAGER substantially improves frozen LLMs as standalone fake news detectors in data-efficient settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/SenticNet/MAGER.

</details>


### [255] [The Uneven Impact of Generative AI on Student Learning: Examining the Roles of Reliance, Evaluation Literacy, and Course Policy in AI-related Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18676)
*Lydia Manikonda,Mei Si,Sirajam Munira,Oshani Seneviratne,Kristin Bennett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于118名学生的调查，识别出四类GenAI使用群体，并揭示早期依赖、认知依赖、评估素养及教师政策等因素与学习收益感知的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI正改变学生学习方式，但课程情境、认知依赖、评估素养与早期依赖等变量的作用尚缺乏充分探讨。

Method: 对12门AI相关课程的118名学生进行问卷调查，采用聚类分析划分用户群体，并结合多变量回归模型检验各因素与感知学习收益的关系。

Result: 识别出四类用户：高使用高收益、低使用低依赖低收益，以及两类收益水平不同的中等使用群体；免费与付费用户、单一与多工具用户、不同教师政策下的学生间存在显著差异。学业收益与早期依赖和学业任务支持相关；正向影响与认知依赖、学业任务支持、对GenAI可靠性的信心及教师政策相关；负向影响与早期依赖和态度变化相关；评估素养越高，早期依赖与负向影响的关联越强。

Conclusion: GenAI增强学习的感知体现认知、绩效与自我效能收益，而对压力和批判性思维弱化的担忧则与较低的学习收益感知相关；机构需制定更完善的政策以应对不平等，帮助学生从日益强大的AI系统中获益。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) is changing how students learn, yet the roles of course context, cognitive reliance, evaluation literacy, and early reliance remain underexplored. Using survey responses from 118 students across 12 AI-related courses at our institution, we examined differences in GenAI use and perceived learning experiences. We identified four user clusters: high-use students reporting many benefits, light users reporting less reliance and fewer benefits, and two moderate-use groups reporting different levels of benefit. We also found significant differences between free- and premium-version users, single- and multiple-tool users, and students experiencing different instructor policies. In multivariable regression models, academic benefit was associated with early reliance and academic task support; positive impact was associated with cognitive reliance, academic task support, confidence in GenAI reliability, and instructor policy; and negative impact was associated with early reliance and attitudinal change. The association between early reliance and negative impact became stronger as evaluation literacy increased. Finally, perceptions of GenAI-enhanced learning appear to reflect cognitive, performance, and self-efficacy benefits, while concerns about stress and diminished critical thinking are associated with lower perceived learning benefits. These findings suggest that institutions need better policies to address such inequities so that institutions can enable students to benefit from increasingly capable AI systems.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond Truncation: Rethinking LLM Decoding as Ensemble Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18723)
*Dunyao Xue,Chengshuo Du,Zhengbo Wang,Wenlin Dai,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ME-Decoding，一种将大语言模型解码中的候选词选择建模为集成剪枝问题的新框架，利用马氏距离驱动的子集优化目标在保持高概率的同时提升语义多样性，并给出近线性复杂度的贪心选择算法及理论近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码选择策略主要依赖标量概率，忽视候选词之间的几何语义关系，导致候选冗余；而现有几何感知方法通常需要复杂优化或直接对原始词概率重新加权，带来较大计算开销或推理不稳定。

Method: 将解码形式化为子集优化问题，采用马氏距离驱动的目标函数，在保留高概率的同时增强语义多样性；通过基于词嵌入的自适应带宽核构建词相似度矩阵，动态折扣冗余生成路径；并设计具有早停机制的近线性复杂度贪心选择算法，同时建立其理论近似保证。

Result: 在多种推理与生成任务上的大量实验表明，该方法持续取得强劲性能；同时作为即插即用模块，推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: ME-Decoding 通过几何感知的集成剪枝式解码，在低开销下兼顾概率质量与语义多样性，是一种稳健且易于集成的解码方案。

Abstract: We introduce Mahalanobis-Ensemble Decoding (ME-Decoding), a novel Large Language Model (LLM) decoding framework that frames candidate token selection as ensemble pruning. Existing selection strategies rely predominantly on scalar probabilities, ignoring geometric semantic relationships and causing candidate redundancy. Meanwhile, current geometry-aware methods often require complex optimization or directly reweighting the original token probabilities, leading to significant computational overhead or inference instability. To address this, we formulate decoding as a subset optimization problem using a Mahalanobis distance-driven objective to enhance semantic diversity while preserving high probabilities. Specifically, we dynamically discount redundant generation paths using a token similarity matrix, constructed via an adaptive-bandwidth kernel over token embeddings. We further devise an efficient greedy selection algorithm with near-linear complexity in the candidate size under early stopping, while establishing its theoretical approximation guarantees. This renders ME-Decoding a robust, plug-and-play module with negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments across diverse reasoning and generation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [257] [Which LLM is Best for Translating Natural Language Goals to PDDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18731)
*Tomas Balyo,Lukas Chrpa,G. Michael Youngblood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文实证评估当前主流大语言模型能否将游戏测试者用非正式自然语言表述的测试目标可靠转换为规范的PDDL规划目标，并系统比较六个先进模型在正确性、速度与错误倾向上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动规划中，用PDDL等形式化语言表达目标对非专家构成门槛，自然语言意图与机器执行之间存在鸿沟；论文旨在检验LLM能否作为弥合该鸿沟的桥梁。

Method: 设计并采用经迭代实验优化的提示模板，针对真实、领域特定的基准，对六个当代先进LLM进行系统评估，从正确性、响应速度和错误倾向三个维度开展实证测试。

Result: 所有模型正确率均超过92%；Gemini 2.5 Flash准确率最高达96%且假阳性最少，GPT-4.1响应速度最快；模型间存在关键性能差异，部分失败源于语言歧义与领域表示局限。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM在连接自然语言目标与自动规划流程方面已取得显著进展，但仍存在持续的能力缺口，尚不能完全胜任稳健的转换桥梁角色。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between human intent and machine execution remains a challenge in automated planning, where expressing goals in formal languages like PDDL restricts accessibility to non-experts. This paper empirically evaluates whether current Large Language Models (LLMs) can reliably translate natural language testing goals, written in informal language by video game testers, into well-formed PDDL targets suitable for classical planning. We present a carefully designed prompt template, integrating insights from iterative experimentation, aimed at maximizing both accuracy and response coherence from multiple state-of-the-art LLMs. Six contemporary models are systematically assessed on correctness, speed, and error tendencies using real-world, domain-specific benchmarks. All models demonstrate high correctness, exceeding 92\%, with Gemini 2.5 Flash achieving the highest accuracy at 96\% and the lowest incidence of false positives, while GPT-4.1 leads in response speed. Despite these advances, critical distinctions exist in model performance, and occasional failures arise from language ambiguity and limitations in domain representation. Our analysis underscores both the significant progress and ongoing gaps in enabling LLMs to act as robust bridges between natural language objectives and automated planning pipelines.

</details>


### [258] [Clueing up LLMs with Tool-Augmented Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18736)
*Rebecca Ansell,Autumn Toney-Wails*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以经典桌游《Clue》构建多智能体文本环境，评测LLM多步演绎推理能力，并提出用结构化可能性矩阵作为工具增强，将隐式游戏状态显式化以减轻智能体的记忆与约束推理负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长程交互中的逻辑一致性演绎推理仍具挑战：跨多步整合证据、与先前推断保持一致、在新约束下更新信念均易暴露短板。此类任务既揭示局限，也提供了评估推理增强手段的测试床，因此需要一个可控的多步、智能体化演绎推理评测环境。

Method: 实现基于文本的多智能体版《Clue》棋盘游戏环境：智能体需从观察序列中推断隐藏信息、跨轮次保持一致性并对演化中的逻辑约束进行推理。以GPT-4o-mini与Gemini-2.5-Flash各三名（共六名）构成玩家进行回合制对局，通过重复对局建立基线表现。随后引入工具增强方法：用结构化“可能性矩阵”将生成推理日志中的隐式游戏状态转化为剩余可能性的显式表征，编码扩展轮次记忆与演绎约束，从而把记忆与约束推理任务从智能体卸载。

Result: 建立了六智能体、两模型族在重复对局中的基线表现，并将工具增强方法与基线直接对比，用以评估工具增强对自主智能体在策略推理环境中的推理质量与任务成功率的支持效果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将隐式状态显式化并以工具形式卸载记忆与约束维护，是提升LLM智能体长程演绎推理一致性与任务表现的可行路径，而多智能体桌游环境可作为评估此类推理增强的有效测试床。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), performing logically consistent deductive reasoning over extended interactions remains challenging. Tasks that require integrating evidence across multiple reasoning steps, maintaining consistency with prior inferences, and updating beliefs under new constraints can surface limitations in current models while providing a useful testbed for evaluating reasoning enhancements. In this paper, we implement a text-based, multi-agent version of the classic board game Clue as an environment to evaluate multi-step, agentic deductive reasoning. In this setting, agents must infer hidden information from a sequence of observations, maintain consistency across turns, and reason over an evolving set of logical constraints. We instantiate six LLM-based agents (GPT-4o-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash) as players that engage in turn-based gameplay; using three agents per model family, we establish baseline performance across repeated games. We then introduce a tool-augmented approach in which a structured possibility matrix converts implicit game state from generated reasoning logs into an explicit representation of remaining possibilities. The possibility matrix encodes extended-turn memory and deductive constraints, offloading these tasks from the agent. We compare this approach against the baseline to evaluate how tool augmentation supports reasoning quality and task success for autonomous agents in a strategic reasoning environment.

</details>


### [259] [Version- and Scope-Aware Question Answering over Normative Documents: A Deployed System and an End-to-End Evaluation at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18769)
*Liuyin Wang,Shuaipeng Jin,Jiwei Shi,Jensen Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在约7.3万份候选规范文件上，用200题分层抽样基准对比：托管检索服务得分88.1，而通过显式规则先解析版本与适用范围的受治理系统得分97.7，相差9.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答基于规范性文件的问题往往依赖单一段落之外的信息：检索到的文件是否为现行有效版本、是否适用于该司法辖区/主体/日期，以及每条规范性主张能否溯源到支撑文本。托管式检索服务大幅降低了搭建初始系统的工程成本，使"上传文档即可提问"成为常见默认做法，因此有必要评估这一默认做法的可靠性。

Method: 在生产部署所供给的约73,000份候选规范文件上进行评测。使用发表于自有基准的200道题的分层抽样，每题均有金标准来源文件；公布的抽样规则不读取任何系统输出或评分。将托管服务与受治理系统进行对比，后者在生成前通过显式规则解析版本与适用范围。

Result: 受治理系统总分97.7，托管服务88.1，按未取整均值计算差距为9.6分。

Conclusion: 问题集、两套系统的被评答案文本、分数及复现基准统计的脚本均已公开。该受治理配置自2026年1月起作为商业产品运营，服务1,126名注册用户；截至2026年4月中旬，已达每工作日约10万次调用。

Abstract: Correctly answering a question grounded in normative documents often depends on information outside any single passage: whether the retrieved document is the version currently in force; whether it applies to the jurisdiction, subject (such as an institution or applicant), and date at issue; and whether each normative claim can be traced to its supporting source text. Hosted retrieval services have substantially lowered the engineering cost of building an initial system over such corpora, making "upload the documents and ask" a common default. We evaluate this default on approximately 73,000 candidate normative documents supplied to a production deployment. The evaluation uses a stratified sample of 200 questions from our published benchmark, with a gold source document for every question; the released sampling rule reads no system outputs or scores. We compare the hosted service with a governed system that resolves version and scope through explicit rules before generation. The governed system scored 97.7 overall, while the hosted service scored 88.1, a gap of 9.6 points computed from unrounded means. The question set, the answer text evaluated for both systems, the scores, and the scripts used to reproduce the reported benchmark statistics are public. The governed configuration has operated as a commercial product since January 2026 and serves 1,126 registered users; named customer organizations include Zhipu AI and Lecheng Health. By mid-April 2026, it had reached roughly 100,000 calls per workday.

</details>


### [260] [CERA-MoA: Co-Evolving Routing Mechanisms with Continually Learning LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18779)
*Jiaxuan Jiang,Liyuan He,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CERA-MoA，一个路由与智能体策略协同演化的迭代强化学习框架，通过预测式熟悉度估计与累积阈值自适应路由，实现动态最小智能体子集激活与能力分化，性能优于静态路由与固定工作流微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Mixture-of-Agents 范式通常将查询路由与智能体微调视为相互分离的流程，导致路由策略无法在后训练阶段适应智能体不断变化的能力，智能体也无法实现数据驱动的协同专业化，两者难以应对演化中的能力变化。因此需要一种路由与智能体共同演化的机制。

Method: 提出 CERA-MoA（Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents），一个动态路由器和独立智能体策略协同演化的迭代强化学习框架。设计预测式熟悉度估计器（predictive familiarity estimator），利用中间层隐藏状态评估智能体间的语义胜任度，避免完整 rollout 的开销；基于熟悉度得分，采用累积阈值自适应路由机制（cumulative-threshold adaptive routing），动态激活定制的最小智能体子集，以权衡任务性能与效率；并根据智能体不断演化的能力，主动分配有针对性的训练样本，促进能力分化。

Result: 在多领域的大量实验中，CERA-MoA 优于当前最先进的静态智能体路由方法与固定工作流微调基线。

Conclusion: 通过路由与智能体策略的协同演化，并结合高效的熟悉度估计与自适应子集路由，CERA-MoA 有效提升了性能与效率的平衡，并推动智能体的能力专业化，证明协同演化范式优于解耦式路由与微调方案。

Abstract: Current Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) paradigms generally treat query routing and agent fine-tuning as separate processes, limiting their ability to respond to evolving agent capabilities. This disconnect prevents routing strategies from adapting to evolving agent capabilities during post-training and prevents agents from achieving synergistic data-driven specialization. To resolve this, we introduce CERA-MoA (Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents), an iterative reinforcement learning framework where the dynamic router and independent agent policies co-evolve. We design a predictive familiarity estimator that leverages mid-layer hidden states to evaluate semantic competence among agents, avoiding the overhead of full rollouts. Based on these familiarity scores, a cumulative-threshold adaptive routing mechanism dynamically activates a tailored minimal agent subset, achieving a trade-off between task performance and efficiency. By proactively allocating targeted training samples to agents based on their evolving competence, CERA-MoA promotes capability differentiation. Extensive experiments across various domains demonstrate that CERA-MoA outperforms state-of-the-art static-agent routing and fix-workflow fine-tuning baselines.

</details>


### [261] [Compositional Policy Violations: When Step-Level Compliance Fails In Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18820)
*Ashwini Kurady,Sri Sai Charith Grandhi,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Mamoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了一类新失效模式——组合式策略违规（CPV），即每个步骤都通过检查、但整体执行违反组织级策略的情形，并提出四类分类法及基于溯源感知的运行时架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic工作流的治理几乎全是"步骤级"的：输入输出分类器、单轮护栏、跨度级评估器。但组织真正持有的策略（转介阈值、权限上限、审查要求）属于整个执行过程的属性，而非任何单一步骤的属性。这种错配导致一个失败模式：每一步各自合规，组合起来却违规。作者试图指出，步骤级监测器再怎么提升精度，也无法检测这类违规，因为单步谓词无法评估该步骤本身无法决定的属性。

Method: 作者将CPV形式化为"步骤级合规无法组合"的失败，并提出包含四类的分类法：Authority Creep（权限蔓延）、Threshold Laundering（阈值洗白）、Cumulative Sum Violation（累积和违规）、Context Collapse（上下文坍塌）。论文论证每一类的正确修复方式由其被守护量（guarded quantity）发生变更的位置所决定，进而引入一种溯源感知（provenance-aware）的运行时架构，在完整执行轨迹上评估策略，从原始溯源重算被守护量，而非依赖流水线的派生表示。

Result: 作者给出了CPV的形式化定义与四类分类法，论证了修复策略与被守护量变更位置之间的对应关系，并提出一种溯源感知运行时架构：在完整执行轨迹上评估策略，并从原始溯源重新计算被守护量。

Conclusion: 步骤级合规无法自动组合，CPV是无法通过提升步骤级监测器精度来检测的独立失效类别。治理架构必须从步骤级转向轨迹级：在完整执行轨迹上评估策略，并依托原始溯源重算被守护量，才能正确检测与修复此类违规。

Abstract: Agentic workflows now make consequential decisions in regulated settings, and the governance placed around them is almost entirely step-scoped: input-output classifiers, per turn rails, and span-level evaluators. The policies organizations actually hold, such as referral thresholds, authority limits, and review requirements, are properties of the whole execution rather than of any one step. This mismatch admits a failure mode we call a Compositional Policy Violation (CPV): every individual step passes its own check while the composed execution violates the governing policy. A predicate over a single step cannot evaluate a property that step does not determine, so no improvement in the accuracy of the step-scoped monitors detects this class. We define CPVs as the failure of step-level compliance to compose, and present a taxonomy of four types: Authority Creep, Threshold Laundering, Cumulative Sum Violation, and Context Collapse. We show that the correct repair for each class is dictated by where the guarded quantity mutates. We then introduce a provenance-aware runtime architecture that evaluates policies over complete execution traces, recomputing guarded quantities from raw provenance rather than the pipeline's derived representation.

</details>


### [262] [Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842)
*Jinli Hu,Ross M. Clarke,Yichuan Zhang,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"无限参数LLM"（Infinite-Parameter LLM）：用一个紧凑的超网络将运行时数据转化为共享基网络的低秩调制，从而把现场交互数据写入权重；并对生成器隐码维持贝叶斯信念并在线更新，使有效权重随会话演化。存储占用固定，可编译的权重本质无限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律与MoE架构依赖静态预训练数据，但部署模型面对的是动态世界：真正提升其有用性的数据（用户提供的事实、给出的纠正等）往往不在训练集中，而在当前实时交互里。传统模型权重冻结，无法从这些数据学习，只能把运行时知识放进提示词（检索或指令），每次请求重新读取、请求结束即丢弃。因此作者追问：架构如何通过把实时交互写入权重来从中学习。

Method: 受MoE启发，提出Infinite-Parameter LLM。用紧凑超网络将运行时给定数据转化为共享基网络的低秩调制，使前馈权重由实时数据生成而非存于固定参数库；与以往只读一次上下文即冻结的权重生成器不同，本文对生成器隐码维持贝叶斯信念并在线上更新，有效权重随会话推进由演化信念重新导出，而非一次读取后固定。

Result: 存储footprint保持固定，而模型可编译的权重实际上趋于无限。对运行时提供的知识与行为，将其承载于权重而非提示词，在计算上被摊还、释放上下文窗口、跨轮次持续存在，并且泛化性可能优于上下文内使用。作者还设计了评估协议，专门对照上下文学习与检索进行检验。

Conclusion: 将运行时交互数据写入权重（而非提示词）是一条可行且有吸引力的路线：固定存储即可获得"无限"可编译权重，兼具计算摊还、上下文解放、跨轮持久与更好泛化等优势。后续需以所提评估协议在上下文学习和检索基线上验证该架构。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [263] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用线性探针直接检测知识编辑后的残留知识：在 GPT-2-XL 上，三类机制不同的编辑器（ROME、受限微调、GRACE）在行为编辑全部成功时，原始宾语仍以远高于随机水平的线性可解码性存留于隐藏状态中，表明编辑是抑制而非擦除原关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑基准只认证"局部正确性"，即编辑后模型能否对近编辑提示输出新事实，却不衡量原始事实在模型内部还有多少可被解码。作者希望直接研究这种残留知识（residual knowledge），检验行为成功背后原有联想是否真正被移除。

Method: 采用线性痕迹探针（linear trace probe）：编辑事实后，从模型隐藏状态中检验原始宾语是否仍可线性恢复。在 GPT-2-XL 上，对 50 条 CounterFact 编辑，分别应用三种机制迥异的编辑器（ROME、受限微调、基于记忆的 GRACE），并对比探针准确率与随机水平（0.50），同时以基于生成的编辑成功率作为对照。另报告一个重学习节省（relearning-savings）工具。

Result: 所有编辑均达到 100% 基于生成的编辑成功。但原始宾语在线性探针下仍显著高于随机水平可解码：ROME 0.96、受限微调 0.86、GRACE 0.79，随机水平 0.50。GRACE 最具信息量：它不改变任何基座模型权重，仅通过外部记忆覆盖事实，原始宾语仍可从底层网络解码，说明残留痕迹不能归因于不完整的权重更新。重学习节省工具在本设置下表现不可靠，被作为负面的方法学结果而非证据报告。

Conclusion: 知识编辑即使在行为上成功，也只是在表征空间中抑制而非擦除原始关联；行为层面的编辑成功不足以证明知识被真正移除。基于记忆、不改变权重的编辑同样保留可解码的原始痕迹，提示局部正确性基准存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [264] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"任务加权图表"(task-weighted charts)，将"语言模型计算用了多少维"这一原本不适定问题转化为可良定义的问题：在指定泛函及其自身度量下用低维坐标拟合表示。跨三个家族六个模型(70M–7B)实验发现，维持5%困惑度内需保留残差流70–90%宽度，且该宽度由语言稀有尾部消耗；激活方差分布无法预测功能维度。维度随泛函而异并随深度增长，基于功能度量的图表压缩优于方差型或最优线性压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语言模型计算实际使用多少维度"这一问题在未指定泛函前是不适定的，因为维度依赖于所考察的功能。作者意在为这一维度问题建立良定义的度量框架，并揭示功能性维度与方差维度之间的解离现象。

Method: 提出任务加权图表(task-weighted charts)：针对选定泛函，在其自身度量下用低维坐标系拟合表示，将蒸馏问题转化为普通最小二乘。在三个模型家族、70M至7B参数的六个模型上进行实验，比较保留宽度与困惑度、分析激活方差谱、考察模型自身不确定性与预测分布所需维度，并对比功能度量图表与方差型/最优线性压缩。

Result: 下一词预测需保留残差流70–90%宽度才能维持在5%困惑度内；该宽度主要由语言稀有尾部消耗；激活方差谱完全无法预测功能所需维度——GPT-2两个方向承载90%激活方差却几乎不承载功能。模型自身不确定性仅需六个坐标即可读取，而完整预测分布需数百维；功能维度随深度增长。压缩对比中，功能度量下训练的图表在只保留少数维度时比方差型或最优线性压缩更好地保持模型预测。

Conclusion: 维度是相对于泛函而言的，方差维度与功能维度存在明确解离。任务加权图表为语言模型功能性维度提供了良定义且可操作的度量方式，并可实际应用于更优的降维与蒸馏。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [265] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出五项任务诊断实验，将多模态大模型在物理与几何推理上的失败分解为感知失败、推理失败或二者兼有，并揭示失败传递具有领域依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型虽在科学推理基准上表现较强，但大多将感知与推理视为单一可测过程，无法定位失败究竟源于图文理解还是推理环节，因此需要解耦二者以诊断真实瓶颈。

Method: 设计跨越物理与几何基准的五项任务诊断实验，通过对比原始图像、人工撰写标题（caption）以及修正标题等输入条件，将模型错误归因于感知、推理或两者，并追踪感知失败后随之出现的推理错误类型。

Result: 错误的图表解读会降低模型表现，即使该题仅凭文本模型本可正确解答；准确率总体从原始图像到人工标题呈上升趋势。部分模型在修正标题下恢复率较高，可区分"感知受阻"型失败与真正的推理性瓶颈。物理失败多转化为计算错误，几何失败则表现为概念性误用。此外，InternS1-mini 尽管经过大量科学预训练并具备思考能力，在所有任务上均低于实验中最弱模型，且推理轨迹常未完成即被截断。

Conclusion: 感知与推理应被解耦评估：感知失败会传播并污染推理结果，且其下游错误形式随领域而异；同时，科学预训练与思维链能力并不必然保证稳健的多模态科学推理表现。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [266] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Gauntlet"开发-冻结-评估框架：让通用编码智能体在仅有游戏描述、原始观测/动作接口和空策略文件的条件下，自主实验单场会话并编写独立控制器，冻结后在留出实例上（对局期间零模型调用）评测，在程序化roguelike、星际争霸II和Freeciv上取得突破性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往LLM智能体即使被研究人员围绕模型搭建感知、记忆、技能库、规划器等脚手架，仍难以将游戏知识转化为有竞争力的实战表现；同时编码智能体的快速进展引出两个更尖锐的问题：前沿模型现在能否赢游戏，以及能否在无辅助下自行构建完整玩家。作者据此提出用"最简契约"检验端到端的自主游戏能力。

Method: Gauntlet框架采用开发-冻结-评估三阶段流程，覆盖从小型街机到全规模商业游戏。契约刻意保持极简：通用编码智能体仅获得游戏描述、原始观测/动作接口和空策略文件，不含任何策略、算法或架构提示。智能体在单次自主会话中与实时游戏交互实验，自行工程化出一个独立控制器；随后冻结该结果，并在留出实例上评分，游玩期间完全不进行模型调用。

Result: 在未公开的程序化roguelike任务上，留出成功率跨度为0%至86%，并显现明显的代际门槛：最新一代系统的每个观测会话都超过前代最佳会话。在全游戏规模上，一个编译后的原始API控制器击败所有公平的星际争霸II内置AI及两个作弊变体；单会话程序在留出种子上以全面征服赢得完整Freeciv对局。虽然在对抗新手AI时胜率不高，但这是首次有语言智能体系统在本类游戏中独立获胜，无需逐回合模型调用，也无需手工制作的战术层。

Conclusion: 前沿编码智能体开始展现出长时程策略能力。冻结后的程序可被审查，作者将这种能力称为"compiled agency"（编译式能动性）：将开发经验编译为持久可执行的智能体，其架构由模型自行构建。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [267] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MUSE基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中的艺术图像理解能力，涵盖12项任务与多文化艺术图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态理解上进步显著，但在教育场景中的能力评估不足。AI辅助语言学习需模型解读艺术图像及其语义、情感与文化内容并推理视觉语境，而现有基准多聚焦真实图像或特定领域教育推理，对艺术教育内容覆盖有限。

Method: 构建MUSE基准，将图像标注与问题生成解耦，实现多样化、难度可控的任务并降低标注成本。包含12项任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解、组合推理，并精选以新加坡及东南亚多元文化语境为主、兼顾西方艺术传统的艺术图像，涵盖多主题与多难度层级。

Result: 对开源与专有模型的评估揭示了各能力维度间的显著差异，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面。分析进一步识别出常见失败模式及开发可信教育多模态模型的关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE有望成为推动情境化教育应用中多模态理解研究的标准化基准。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [268] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Flag Game玩具模型研究AI智能体集体信念形成机制，刻画了集体信念崩塌向极化的转变，并结合社会回路归因与统计力学理论揭示其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的涌现协调行为带来关键安全风险，其驱动力是关于世界的信念快速形成与传播，因此理解集体信念形成的机制对集体对齐至关重要，而现有研究缺乏相应的机制性理解工具。

Method: 提出Flag Game玩具模型：隐藏国旗定义真值，每个有限智能体仅直接观察私有局部图像，但可交换信念并权衡来自同伴的社会证据。采用两种互补方法：一是社会回路归因（social circuit attribution），预测哪个智能体及何种观点对集体动态最重要，并通过智能体因果干预验证；二是针对大规模群体发展统计力学理论。

Result: Flag Game复现了丰富的集体现象：性能随群体规模的非单调变化、社会感知提示与团队多样性带来的准确率提升、组织结构显著影响。发现小规模群体下的集体信念崩塌随规模增长转为集体信念极化，极化导致大规模下性能下降但形成信念多样性。因果干预效果随群体增大而下降，统计力学理论与实证相图吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作迈出了机制性群体可解释性（mechanistic swarm interpretability）的第一步，即研究个体智能体属性及其通信如何产生涌现集体行为的科学。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [269] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过为SwiftSage增加自适应记忆模块(AMM)与自我反思模块(SRM)两个模块化认知扩展，在ScienceWorld上消融实验表明完整系统取得最优表现，执行时控制是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互环境中仍表现脆弱：需要长时程状态跟踪、有效动作执行以及失败步骤的恢复。为此需要为双过程智能体引入记忆与反思能力以提升交互鲁棒性。

Method: 扩展双过程智能体SwiftSage（快速动作提议器+慢速规划器），加入两个以特征开关实现的模块化扩展：自适应记忆模块AMM（显著性门控的情节存储与触发式检索）和自我反思模块SRM（有界执行时验证与纠正性干预）。两模块共用同一执行底座，支持受控消融。在ScienceWorld上进行四种配置对比：baseline、baseline+AMM、baseline+SRM、完整系统。

Result: 完整系统取得最佳平均最终得分64.62、成功率43.17%、成功步数效率19.33步；SRM是单独贡献最强的模块。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，执行时控制是主导性瓶颈，而情节记忆在运行时循环趋于稳定后才发挥最大作用。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把模型更新上线前的风险比较形式化为"成对风险差异审计"，提出利用两模型分歧位于无标签可观测输入上的support identity，设计两层DISCERN协议：零标签层用无标签流量认证分歧率低于容差的良性更新，审计层通过anytime-valid置信序列仅对有分歧样本打标，并给出有限样本有效性与ρ²/ε²量级的标签复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型因重训练、微调、量化或静默替换而不断更新，每次更新都可能比被替换的模型更差。现有审计方法多为无标签盲配对或缺乏随时有效的统计保证，无法在有限错误预算下连续认证多个更新，因此需要一种既能利用免费的分歧信号、又对任意停止时刻与对抗性判官都成立的成对风险差异审计框架。

Method: 1) 提出support identity：两模型风险差异仅集中在其预测不一致的输入上，且该集合无需标签即可观测。2) 构建DISCERN两层序列协议：零标签层仅凭无标签流量认证分歧率低于容差的良性更新；审计层仅对采样到的分歧样本打标，采用anytime-valid置信序列，对任意停止时刻和任意打标路径（含对抗性判官）均有效。3) 证明有限样本有效性及速率层面ρ²/ε²量级的匹配标签复杂度界，表明利用免费分歧信息相比任何配对盲审计者可证明节省1/ρ倍标签，且该保证可在单一错误预算下跨无界次数的更新序列复合。

Result: 在785个更新对（含最高1.4B参数语言模型的LoRA微调）上重放超过14,000条审计流：误覆盖率0.0002（标称5%），功效0.986且零误报，56%的良性更新在零标签条件下即被认证。每次审计输出可供机器校验的证据记录，用于上市后监控。

Conclusion: 该工作将更新上线决策转化为可证明有效的成对风险差异审计问题，通过分歧支撑恒等式和两层随时有效协议，在严格统计保证下显著降低标签成本并支持连续、可复合的模型更新认证；实证结果验证了极低误覆盖、高功效、零误报与高比例的零标签认证，并为后市场监控提供可校验证据。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在T4等低显存GPU上部署RAG存在“压缩悖论”：神经提示压缩的KV缓存争用与预处理延迟可能超过生成收益，而不压缩则长上下文OOM。作者提出Tri-Metric Router，一种确定性、免训练的路由策略，在Raw、Neural(LLMLingua-2)、Lexical(BM25)三种管道间基于硬件物理信号选择。校准后T4交叉点约4,332词，实现0% OOM、88.5±4.4% oracle对齐、49.3% Combined F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在16GB显存的NVIDIA T4等商用GPU上部署RAG时存在两难：启用神经提示压缩会引入KV缓存争用和预处理延迟，抵消生成阶段的时间节省；而完全跳过压缩又会在长上下文场景触发OOM。现有方法多做语义层面的自适应，缺少从硬件物理（显存余量）角度出发的调度依据，因此需要一个能兼顾显存约束与延迟的确定性路由方案。

Method: 作者识别出vLLM服务的LLM与PyTorch压缩器在紧显存预算下共部署时的两种失效机制，并设计Tri-Metric Router：一种确定性、免训练的策略，在Raw、Neural(LLMLingua-2)、Lexical(BM25)三种管道中选择。路由使用三个CPU侧信号：空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key、类符形符比TTR。与以往仅依赖语义的自适应不同，其调度信号基于硬件物理，依据VRAM余量与延迟交叉点。阈值在LongBench qasper上profiling校准，T4上运行交叉点约4,332词。贡献在于校准方法本身而非硬件特定常数。

Result: 在分布外（OOD）留出集上，方法实现0% OOM失败、88.5±4.4%的oracle对齐率、49.3%的Combined F1，相比always-on词法压缩提升5.2个百分点，且不增加额外VRAM或训练成本。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了低显存GPU上RAG提示压缩的“压缩悖论”及其两种失效机制，提出基于硬件物理信号的确定性免训练路由Tri-Metric Router。通过可复用的profiling校准方法确定延迟交叉点，在OOD场景下同时消除OOM并提升F1，证明硬件感知的压缩策略选择比语义自适应或固定压缩更有效。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [272] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"Transition Games"（行为对齐的精确激活博弈，并配有配对的非泛化对照），用于定位Transformer中从记忆到泛化转变的功能表达位置。研究发现这是一种已有分布式电路的谱重编码，而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer中从记忆（memorization）到泛化（generalization，此处即grokking）的转变究竟在何处被功能性地表达，并检验流行的"MLP负责记忆、注意力负责泛化"假说是否成立。

Method: 引入Transition Games：行为对齐的精确激活博弈（exact activation games），并配备成对的非泛化对照组。通过替换博弈（replacement games）分析二级模式（degree-two modes）的贡献；用不重叠的精确路径研究（exact path study）比较block-1 MLP与其他下游路径的中介效应；同时检验路由起始（routing onset）、全局秩坍塌（global rank collapse）与素数不变架构脊（prime-invariant architecture ridge）等假说。

Result: 发现分布式效用增益，并伴有前瞻性的block-0注意力偏置；选定的二级模式在替换博弈中解释了其加法对比度的67–92%。独立的精确路径研究显示，在12/12配对中block-1 MLP比所有其他受测下游路径中介了更多效应。"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"这一更尖锐的预测反而被推翻（在记忆锚点处为−.331 bits/example；12配对中0个方向符合预测）。路由起始、全局秩坍塌及素数不变架构脊也均未通过检验。

Conclusion: 此处的grokking应被理解为已有分布式电路的谱重编码（spectral recoding），而非模块间的功能切换。经典的"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"二分法不成立。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [273] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究审计CLIP模型在社会性别偏见上的表现，发现粗粒度零样本评分显示性别等价，但归因于指标灵敏度不足与档案生存偏差，而非模型绝对公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉-语言模型（VLM）的社会偏见时，需区分算法层面的直接估值差异与档案元数据中的混杂因素；既有评估常忽略混杂控制与档案来源问题。

Method: 使用大都会艺术博物馆开放获取藏品的艺术品元数据（N=1,500；具名作品N=743：男534、女209；匿名618），对OpenAI CLIP与OpenCLIP进行零样本logit差分评分审计，覆盖masterpiece、quality、influence三组语义提示；采用双单侧检验（TOST）做等价性检验（Cohen's d≥0.25），并用多元OLS回归控制媒介、创作年代与宽高比。

Result: 未调整评估显示两位模型均无显著性别主效应（OpenAI CLIP：mu_F=-0.0067 vs mu_M=-0.0035，p=0.1829；OpenCLIP：mu_F=0.0171 vs mu_M=0.0237，p=0.1224）；TOST确认在d≥0.25界内统计等价（pTOST<0.005）。多元OLS回归（R²<0.02）显示艺术家性别无显著条件效应（p>0.20）。残差嵌入方差高（R²<2%），表明全局零样本估值指标接近嵌入噪声底。

Conclusion: 宏观评分等价反映指标对细粒度视觉-语义特征不敏感，不能排除局部微观视觉偏见；排除41.2%未署名藏品带来机构生存偏差。评估文化遗产AI公平性时必须进行多元混杂控制、等价性检验与档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [274] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Temperon 训练调度：前 43% 用普通 SGD 探索，剩余整个余弦退火阶段交给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修器，在保持全时 SAM 精度的同时显著降低时间成本，并给出可迁移的分配规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM（锐度感知最小化）会使每步训练成本翻倍，但其收益集中在训练末期。作者希望研究“昂贵的训练模式应投入在何处”，从而在有限算力下最大化收益。

Method: Temperon：早期（43% epoch 预算）使用 plain-SGD 探索，随后一次计划性切换（hand-off），将整个最终余弦退火阶段交给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修器。并做消融实验、与 late-phase SAM 对比，并迁移到 GPT-2 预训练与 GLUE 微调。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet（5 个种子）上，Temperon 精度匹配最佳全时 SAM 配方，并在四个数据集中的三个上更快达到最难常见目标（分别提前 35%、34%、32%）；Muon 精修器单独贡献 +0.85pp；探索器形状与重启无贡献（已撤回）。late-phase SAM 在中期目标上最快，但无法达到 Muon 精修器带来的精度层级（CIFAR-100 0.83、CIFAR-10 0.97）；Tiny ImageNet 上 Muon 未带来层级提升，对手胜出。迁移到 GPT-2 预训练（全 SAM 质量、-29% 墙钟时间）与 GLUE 微调（不差于全时 SAM，SAM 成本仅为其 1/3）。

Conclusion: 训练模式应按阶段分配：早期跳过 SAM 获得固定“信用”，Muon 一个 epoch 成本为 SAM+SGD 的 1.50x（四数据集一致）。切换点无法从轨迹中定时，因余弦调度下精度曲线呈平台后激增，信息存在于调度本身，故计划性切换具有原理性而非仅便利。代码与 pip 实现已发布。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [275] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了基于梯度的训练能否学习矩阵记忆中存储与组合联想所需的秩。实验表明，学习到的有效秩随绑定数量K增长，秩上限会在k=K附近产生恢复相变，且训练后的算子可多次自应用保持高恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究使用矩阵增强推理器处理允许秩-1解的任务，无法回答梯度训练能否学习到存储和组合联想所需的秩，因此该问题悬而未决。

Method: 在K个全新键-值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，其精确线性恢复要求rank(Z)≥K；采用固定线性读出查询单一矩阵状态，不访问原始绑定；以余弦相似度大于0.9衡量恢复，并设置训练期秩上限。

Result: 学习到的有效秩随K增加（d=16时Spearman ρ=1.0）。秩上限在k=K附近产生恢复相变：d=8、K=4时，秩3恢复≤0.0004，秩4恢复0.97。五个种子中四个在21次自应用中保持≥0.9996恢复。实体子空间上学习算子有效秩接近K并近似理想循环；对低于K上限的收敛种子，预测余弦与实测误差在7次应用内为0.008。延长训练可解决若干初始失败，但固定编码器宽度时更大矩阵维度下恢复仍下降。

Conclusion: 梯度训练能够学习到与所需存储容量相匹配的有效秩，且所学算子具有可组合、可迭代应用的特性；秩上限决定恢复相变，但更大维度下仍存在恢复退化问题。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [276] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究改进型多臂老虎机（improving multi-armed bandits）中的竞争比与先验知识代价问题。作者提出"probe-and-commit"算法，在T≥2⌊√k⌋时无需知道尺度m即可达到4√3√k的竞争比，并确定任意时限下的最优比Θ(√k+k/T)；无噪声时可用随机边际探测算法无需任何先验同时达到最优Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)；乘性噪声下无需知道噪声水平仍保持Θ(√k+k/T)，但自适应代价φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blum与Ravichandran (ALT 2025)证明随机算法在已知尺度m时可达到O(√k)近似，未知时O(√k log k)，而Ω(√k)下界表明对数因子本可消除。同时Blum等 (UAI 2026)引入凹包络指数β等先验参数，使算法依赖过多。本文动机是消除不必要的对数因子、去除对先验知识（尺度m、指数β、时限T、噪声水平）的依赖，并刻画自适应所需付出的代价。

Method: 提出one-page probe-and-commit算法；无噪声情形下提出random-marginal probing算法；噪声情形下提出nested random-permutation probing算法。分析方法包括竞争比上界证明、最优比的下界匹配（Θ记号），以及定义uniform price of adaptation φ_ε(k)并在固定ε下给出渐近上下界。

Result: probe-and-commit在T≥2⌊√k⌋时达到竞争比4√3√k且无需知道尺度m；确定任意时限（含未知时限）最优比为Θ(√k+k/T)；无噪声时无任何先验（不需m、β、T）即可对每个β与每个时限同时达到最优Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)；乘性噪声下probe-and-commit在不知噪声水平时保持全天限阶Θ(√k+k/T)（且在同一范围上为Θ(√k)）；自适应代价φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，由nested random-permutation算法匹配，而仅知m或β之一则可恢复常数代价。

Conclusion: 对数因子可从竞争比中移除，且最优竞争比依赖时限呈Θ(√k+k/T)形式。无噪声时先验完全不必需；噪声模型下算法能免费适应噪声水平，但若同时缺少m与β，自适应需付出φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))的代价，而知悉任一参数即可将代价降为常数。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound. The logarithmic factor is unnecessary: a one-page \emph{probe-and-commit} algorithm achieves competitive ratio $4\sqrt3\,\sqrt k$ for $T\ge2\lfloor\sqrt k\rfloor$, without any knowledge of the scale, and we determine the optimal ratio for every horizon, $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$, also for unknown horizons. Without noise, \emph{no prior is needed at all}: a random-marginal probing algorithm reading neither the scale $m$, nor the concavity-envelope exponent $β$ of Blum, Garicano, Ravichandran and Sharma (UAI 2026), nor the horizon $T$, achieves the optimal $Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)$ simultaneously for every $β$ and every horizon.
