Under the multiplicative noise model of Blum and Ravichandran, probe-and-commit keeps the same all-horizon order $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$ without knowing the noise level (and $Θ(\sqrt k)$ on the same range), but the price of priors jumps: for any fixed noise level $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2]$, the uniform price of adaptation $φ_\varepsilon(k)$ --- the worst case over horizons $T\ge16k$ of the loss relative to $k^{β/(1+β)}$ for algorithms knowing neither $m$ nor $β$ --- is $Θ_\varepsilon(\sqrt{\log k/\log\log k})$, the lower bound asymptotic in $k$ at fixed positive $\varepsilon$ and matched by a nested random-permutation probing algorithm, whereas knowing either $m$ or $β$ alone restores a constant price.

</details>


### [277] [Lecture notes on Physics Informed Neural Networks, Neural Operators, and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17638)
*Alessandro Bombini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文件为博尔扎诺大学2025/2026学年"物理信息神经网络"博士课程讲义，介绍PINN与神经算子的概念、PyTorch从零实现及PhysicsNeMo库应用，并涵盖MoM、FNO、PIKANs等前沿主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向博士课程教学，旨在引入物理信息深度神经网络（PINN）与神经算子（NOs），弥合深度学习与实际工程、物理及油藏问题之间的差距，并提供从理论到实践的系统学习路径。

Method: 以讲义形式组织课程内容：先讲解PINN与NOs的基本概念；通过PyTorch从零实现加深理解；再使用NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等专用开源库解决工程、物理、油藏等真实问题；同时讨论最新研究主题。

Result: 形成一套完整的博士课程讲义，覆盖PINN/NOs基础、PyTorch手工实现、PhysicsNeMo库应用，以及Mixture-of-Models、Fourier Neural Operators、Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs)等前沿方法。

Conclusion: 该讲义为PINN与神经算子的教学与研究提供系统性资源，兼顾理论概念、代码实现与真实场景应用，并跟踪该领域的最新进展。

Abstract: This is the set of lecture notes for the PhD course \href{https://www.unibz.it/en/faculties/engineering/phd-computer-science/study-course-offering/2025/36967}{\textit{Physics Informed Neural Network}, held at the University of Bozen/Bolzano} in the academic year 2025/2026.
  The goal of the course was to introduce the concept of Physics Informed Deep Neural Networks (PINN) and Neural Operators (NOs), discuss their implementation from scratch in PyTorch and using advanced ad-hoc developed open-source libraries such as NVIDia PhysicsNeMo to address real-world problems in various fields (engineering, physics, petroleum reservoir). We discuss recent topics such as Mixture-of-Models, Fourier Neural Operators, Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs) and Fourier Neural Operators.

</details>


### [278] [Fathom: Per-Query Read Depth for Sparse Decoding over Offloaded KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17652)
*Vivek Kalyanarangan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fathom 提出一种按查询自适应决定键各通道读取位数的键扫描方法，将 4-bit K 缓存以位平面（bit plane）通道主序存储，通过逆注水（reverse water-filling）分配位预算，从而降低长上下文解码时的访存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当 agentic 会话达到百万 token 且多会话同时驻留时，KV cache 及其排序索引位于主机内存中，为 top-k 步骤对所有 n 个键进行排序的扫描成为限制解码的访存流量。现有稀疏注意力方案采用固定位宽扫描（如 Double Sparsity、Loki、SparQ），在访存开销与精度之间缺乏灵活的按查询自适应权衡。

Method: 将 4-bit K 缓存按通道主序存储为位平面，使前 t 个平面的前缀恰好对应该通道的 t-bit 量化器；查询根据其各通道方差加权重要性，通过逆注水算法分配位预算，从而决定每个键通道读取多少位，实现按查询自适应的变长键扫描。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 的百万 token 场景下，单次解码步骤的 GPU 时间比 Double Sparsity、Loki 及 SparQ r=32 的 136-bit 扫描快 1.67 倍；在与 SparQ 68-bit 读取（r=16）相同的 GPU 时间内，Fathom 读取字节数少 18%，且在七种模型与上下文设置中的六种上注意力误差更低。在 RULER 类任务上每个逐 token 扫描均与精确 top-k 解码匹配；在真实编程 agent 会话中，Fathom 以 92 位达到最精确 136-bit 扫描的步一致率。

Conclusion: Fathom 可直接复用量化服务栈已持有的 4-bit K 副本作为存储，无需额外索引存储开销。该方法在索引驻留于 GPU 内存时并不带来加速，说明其收益主要来自主机内存访存流量的降低。

Abstract: When agentic sessions run to a million tokens with many sessions resident at once, the KV cache and the index that ranks it live in host memory, and the scan that ranks all n keys for a top-k step becomes the traffic that bounds decoding. We present Fathom, a key scan in which each query decides how many bits of each key channel to read. The 4-bit K cache is stored channel-major as bit planes, so a prefix of t planes is exactly the channel's t-bit quantizer, and the query spends its bit budget by reverse water-filling over the variance-weighted importance of its channels. At one million tokens on Qwen3-8B a decode step is 1.67x faster in GPU time than with the 136-bit scans of Double Sparsity, Loki and SparQ r=32, and in the same GPU time as SparQ's 68-bit read (r=16) Fathom reads 18% fewer bytes with lower attention error on six of seven model and context settings. On RULER-style tasks every per-token scan matches exact top-k decoding, and on real coding-agent sessions Fathom reaches the step agreement of the most accurate 136-bit scan at 92 bits. The store is the 4-bit K copy a quantized serving stack already holds, and the method is not faster when the index is resident in GPU memory.

</details>


### [279] [Reflect, Revise, Reuse: Training-Free Skill Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17653)
*Bofan Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Fei Tang,Zhengxi Lu,Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 EvoSkill-GUI，将 GUI 智能体技能建模为可在部署时经执行反馈修订的动态多文件包，通过"反思-修订-复用"循环持续改进技能，在三个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体需在动态界面上执行长时程任务，弹窗、延迟加载与控件移位常使执行前固定的计划失效。现有 agent-skill 框架虽封装可复用过程性知识，但技能设计未针对 GUI 执行动态，且把技能当作部署前生成的静态产物，而非可在部署中通过执行反馈持续改进的"活的"过程性知识。作者主张所需并非更好的静态技能，而是能在部署时无需额外训练即可依据反馈修订的技能。

Method: EvoSkill-GUI 是一个免训练框架。每项技能是结构化多文件包，包含检索元数据、可执行计划、备份定位、故障恢复规则、无障碍工具以及失败案例。框架运行"反思-修订-复用"循环：执行器在 rollout 中进行即时修订，隔离的 critic 在严格信息隔离下诊断失败轨迹，执行器经受限工具接口编辑特定技能文件。

Result: 在覆盖移动与桌面平台的三类主流 GUI 基准 MobileWorld、AndroidWorld 与 OSWorld 上，EvoSkill-GUI 无需任何训练即可稳定提升多种基座模型，最高增益分别为 +16.2%、+6.0% 与 +10.5%，且演化出的技能库能持续惠及相关任务，而非每次从零重建。

Conclusion: GUI 智能体的关键不在于静态技能的质量，而在于技能能否在部署时依据执行反馈免训练地修订。EvoSkill-GUI 以结构化的多文件技能包与"反思-修订-复用"闭环实现了这一点，在多个跨平台基准上取得一致的性能提升，并展现出技能库跨任务复用与累积的价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: GUI agents execute long-horizon tasks on dynamic graphical user interfaces, where pop-ups, delayed loads, and relocated widgets routinely invalidate plans fixed before execution. Recent agent-skill frameworks encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge to mitigate this, yet existing skill designs are largely developed without targeting GUI execution dynamics and treat skills as static artifacts produced before deployment rather than living procedural knowledge that improves through it. We argue that what GUI agents need is not better static skills, but skills that can be revised from execution feedback at deployment time, without additional training. We propose \textbf{EvoSkill-GUI}, a training-free framework in which each skill is a structured multi-file package containing retrieval metadata, executable plans, backup localization, failure-recovery rules, accessibility utilities, and failure cases. EvoSkill-GUI operates through a \textbf{\emph{reflect-revise-reuse}} loop: the executor performs instant in-rollout revisions, an isolated critic diagnoses failed trajectories under strict information isolation, and the executor edits specific skill files through a restricted tool interface. Across MobileWorld, AndroidWorld, and OSWorld, three mainstream GUI benchmarks spanning mobile and desktop platforms, EvoSkill-GUI consistently improves multiple base models without any training, with maximum gains of $+16.2\%$, $+6.0\%$, and $+10.5\%$ respectively, and evolved skill libraries continue to benefit related tasks rather than being rebuilt from scratch. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/EvoSkill-GUI.

</details>


### [280] [Regularized Least Squares Training of Quadratic Neural Networks with Applications to System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17654)
*Luis Rodrigues,Zachary Yetman Van Egmond,Mohammad R. Amiri Fard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带正则化的二次神经网络最小二乘训练方法，可给出训练优化问题解的下界、近似解的闭式表达及其敏感性，并在非线性系统辨识中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二次神经网络训练多依赖反向传播等迭代数值方法，计算耗时长且易陷入局部极小值，缺乏对解的解析刻画与敏感性分析。

Method: 采用最小二乘框架并结合正则化训练二次神经网络；在正则化系数为正时推导训练优化问题解的下界，给出近似解及其敏感性的闭式表达式；并建立下界优化与核范数最小化之间的联系。

Result: 下界是紧的；当正则化系数为零时，近似解即为最优解。闭式权重表达式显著减少计算时间，避免了迭代方法的局部极小值问题。方法成功应用于非线性系统辨识，并将所提下界与最优值进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该工作有三项主要贡献：(i) 给出权重的解析表达式；(ii) 给出权重对数据误差敏感性的解析表达式；(iii) 建立下界优化与核范数最小化之间的联系。整体上为二次神经网络训练提供了一种高效、可解析的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a least squares approach for the training of quadratic neural networks with regularization. The proposed methodology yields a lower bound on the solution of the training optimization problem for the case where the regularization coefficient is positive. Moreover, it yields closed-form expressions for the approximate solution and its sensitivity The lower bound is tight and the approximate solution is the optimal solution when the regularization coefficient is zero. Having a closed-form expression for the weights reduces considerably the computational time when compared with iterative numerical methods such as backpropagation that can get stuck in local minima. The proposed approach has three main contributions, namely, (i) it yields an analytical expression for the weights, (ii) an analytical expression for the sensitivity of the weights to errors in the data is also provided, (iii) it establishes a connection between the optimization to compute a lower bound and nuclear norm minimization. The proposed least squares training is successfully applied to a nonlinear system identification example where the proposed lower bound is compared with the optimal value.

</details>


### [281] [DSD: Learning Diverse and Reusable Motor Skills via Diffusion Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17682)
*Sun Woo Kim,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD)，利用扩散模型通过score matching逼近策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度，从而学习高维人形控制中多样化、可复用的运动技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过复用丰富的运动技能库高效学习新任务，类似策略可用于模拟角色。为支持广泛下游任务，技能库需具备多样性（不同行为及每个行为的时空变化）。常用方法通过最大化技能隐变量与状态间的互信息来学习多样技能，其中边际状态熵促进行为覆盖，条件熵鼓励一致性。但在高维控制问题中直接估计边际状态熵不可行，现有方法依赖隐空间间接近似或粗糙的状态分布估计，难以有效促进状态空间的广泛覆盖，导致技能行为多样性有限、下游任务效用降低。

Method: 提出Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD)：使用扩散模型，通过score matching近似策略诱导状态分布的熵梯度；由该目标构成的优化函数鼓励发现能产生更广泛行为的高维人形控制技能。学到的技能在两种下游控制设置中复用：带任务特定高层策略的分层控制，以及通过从离线轨迹中进行隐变量选择的零样本控制。

Result: 实验表明，相比先前的技能发现方法，DSD发现了更广泛的可复用运动技能库，涌现出复杂且敏捷的行为，并可在多种下游任务间复用。

Conclusion: DSD通过扩散模型近似状态分布熵梯度，有效克服了高维控制中边际状态熵估计不可行的问题，促进了更广的行为覆盖，学习到多样且可复用的运动技能，在分层控制与零样本控制中均展现出优于先前方法的实用性。

Abstract: Humans efficiently learn new tasks by reusing a rich repertoire of motor skills across different goals and contexts. A similar strategy can also be used to enable simulated characters to efficiently perform new tasks by leveraging reusable motor skills. To support a wide range of downstream tasks, the learned repertoire should be diverse, consisting of distinct behaviors as well as spatial and temporal variation within each behavior. A commonly used method for learning diverse skills is by maximizing the mutual information between skill latents and the states produced by a policy. The marginal state entropy promotes broad behavioral coverage, while the conditional entropy encourages consistent behaviors from each latent. However, directly estimating the marginal state entropy is intractable in high-dimensional control problems. Prior methods therefore rely on indirect latent-space approximations or coarse estimators of the state distribution. These approximations may not effectively promote broad coverage of the state space, resulting in skills with limited behavioral diversity and reduced utility for downstream tasks. In this work, we propose Diffusion Skill Discovery (DSD), a skill discovery method that uses a diffusion model to approximate the entropy gradient of the policy-induced state distribution through score matching. The resulting objective encourages the discovery of skills that produce a broader range of behaviors for high-dimensional humanoid control. The learned skills are reused in two downstream control settings: hierarchical control with a task-specific high-level policy and zero-shot control through latent selection from offline trajectories. Our experiments show that DSD discovers a broader repertoire of reusable motor skills than prior skill discovery methods, leading to the emergence of complex and agile behaviors that can be reused across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [282] [The Missing "I Don't Know": Why Three Reasoning-Reliability Findings Converge on Calibrated Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17686)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 三篇看似无关的LLM可靠性研究（推理RL破坏工具可靠性表征、安全约束下大模型改写/小模型截断、一致性推理系统的幻觉定理）被论证为收敛于同一缺失能力——校准式弃答（calibrated abstention），其根源各异（能力缺口、策略缺口、递归论缺口），而当前排行榜评估对"拒答"零奖励，导致该能力的训练信号缺失。作者提出四项评估改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三篇结果分别记录了三类不同的LLM可靠性失败，表面上互不相关；作者希望揭示其共同点，从而指出一个统一的干预方向，并说明为何该干预在当前评估体系下无法实现。

Method: 概念性/论证性综合分析：将三项实证与理论结果并列解读，追溯各自缺口来源（能力、策略、递归论），并将其归因于同一缺失功能——校准式弃答；进而分析排行榜激励机制对弃答的零奖励，指出训练梯度缺失。

Result: 三项发现均指向校准式弃答为缺失能力；已部署模型经诚实性后训练有所改善，但Bastounis所识别的类别无法被原则性封闭，因为缺乏排行榜层面的训练信号。据此提出四项评估改革：三重计分、弃答率报告、能力分层评估、强制校准指标。

Conclusion: 基准改革对封闭该定理所指缺口是必要的，但不是充分的。

Abstract: Three recent results describe what look like unrelated LLM reliability problems. Yin et al. (2026) show reasoning RL collapses tool-reliability representations. Suleymanov et al. (2026) show that under safety-constrained generation, large models rewrite flagged spans while small models truncate. Bastounis et al. (2024) prove any consistent-reasoning system without an implicit "I don't know" function must hallucinate infinitely often on broad problem classes. We argue these findings converge on a single intervention: calibrated abstention is what each independently identifies as the missing capability, even though the unavailability they document, a capability gap, a policy gap, and a recursion-theoretic gap, has a different source in each case. Honesty post-training has narrowed the gap in deployed models, but principled closure of the class Bastounis identifies requires a calibrated abstention function whose training signal at the leaderboard level is absent: dominant benchmarks assign zero reward to decline, so the leaderboard gradient that would select for the function does not exist. We propose four changes to evaluation: triple-scoring, abstention-rate reporting, capability-stratified evaluation, and mandatory calibration metrics. Benchmark reform is necessary, not sufficient, for closing the gap the theorem identifies.

</details>


### [283] [Accelerating Diffusion Sampling via Speculative Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17691)
*Marcello Bullo,Yanxiao Liu,Öykü Sıla Güner,Arpan Mukherjee,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散模型的投机采样与相对熵编码（REC）建立联系，指出前瞻式草稿不必局限于链式图，进而提出"草稿树"扩展候选集，并采用贪心拒绝采样作为草稿-目标耦合，在保证精确目标分布的前提下最多实现8.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散采样器（尤其是基于反射极大耦合的方法）在拓扑上受限：其前瞻草稿形成链式图（单一线性序列），这从根本上限制了每次目标评估的接受率，导致加速效果有限。作者希望突破这一线性结构约束以提升接受率、减少目标函数评估次数。

Method: 将扩散模型中的投机采样与相对熵编码（REC）建立理论联系；基于该视角提出"草稿树"（draft trees），使前瞻不再局限于线性链式结构，从而丰富每轮候选状态；同时采用REC中的贪心拒绝采样作为草稿-目标耦合机制。

Result: 在多种目标模型与草稿模型上的实验表明，相较于反射耦合基线，该方法在实际场景中最多实现8.3%的加速，同时保证生成样本精确服从目标分布。

Conclusion: 通过REC视角重新审视扩散投机采样，草稿结构可从链式图推广为树状结构，结合贪心拒绝采样耦合，可在保证精确性的同时提升接受率与采样效率，为扩散模型加速提供了新的结构设计思路。

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion model generation by drafting inexpensive candidate states and correcting them under a coupling that preserves the target distribution exactly, reducing the number of expensive target evaluations. Existing diffusion samplers, notably those based on reflection maximal coupling, are topologically constrained: their lookahead drafts form a chain graph, a single linear sequence, which inherently limits the acceptance rate per target evaluation. We connect speculative sampling in diffusion models to relative entropy coding (REC). This perspective shows the lookahead need not be linear and motivates our central contribution, draft trees, which enrich the candidates considered per round and lower the target function evaluations. We further adopt greedy rejection sampling, an REC algorithm, as the draft-target coupling, improving acceptance while guaranteeing exact target samples. Experiments across diverse target and draft models demonstrate up to 8.3% acceleration over the reflection coupling baseline in practical settings.

</details>


### [284] [Composite-Gradient Learning for Shared Control Authority Between Deep Reinforcement Learning and Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17697)
*Giray Önür,Azita Dabiri,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出复合梯度学习（CGL）方法，将MPC控制器显式纳入DRL训练过程，把DRL与MPC控制输入表示为联合动作，并在更新DRL智能体时考虑二者交互。在多类高速公路交通网络上评估，结果表明：弱交互下CGL收益有限，强交互下部分训练轮次中学习到更优控制策略，但平均控制性能提升仍较温和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享控制权的DRL-MPC框架中，DRL智能体与MPC控制器各自决定部分控制输入。然而常见的学习范式将MPC视为环境的一部分，因而既未显式考虑MPC对控制的贡献，也未建模其与DRL智能体的交互，导致学习信号不完整、策略更新偏差。

Method: 提出复合梯度学习（CGL）：将DRL与MPC的控制输入表示为联合动作，并在训练中更新DRL智能体时显式计入两者的交互作用，从而把MPC控制器集成进学习过程，而非仅将其当作环境。在具有不同DRL-MPC交互强度的两个多类高速公路交通网络上进行验证，并与将MPC视为环境、或仅部分纳入MPC的替代方法对比。

Result: 在弱交互条件下，CGL相较替代方法收益有限；在强交互条件下，CGL在一部分训练轮次中学习到控制性能更高的策略，但平均控制性能增益仍属中等（modest）。

Conclusion: 显式建模MPC与DRL控制输入间的交互（联合动作+复合梯度）对共享控制场景是有益的，尤其在交互强度较高时能够提升策略性能；但其平均收益有限，说明该方法的有效性与交互强度密切相关，且存在改进空间。

Abstract: Integrated deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and model predictive control (MPC) methods are increasingly used to control autonomous systems by combining their complementary capabilities. DRL learns control policies through interaction with the environment. MPC uses a system model to optimize control inputs while accounting for constraints. In DRL-MPC frameworks with shared control authority, both the DRL agent and the MPC controller each determine part of the control inputs. However, common learning formulations treat MPC as part of the environment and therefore do not explicitly account for MPC's contribution to control or its interaction with the DRL agent. This paper proposes a novel composite-gradient learning (CGL) method that integrates the MPC controller into the learning process by representing the DRL and MPC control inputs as a joint action and accounting for their interaction when updating the DRL agent during training. CGL is evaluated on two multi-class freeway traffic networks with different strengths of interaction between the DRL and MPC control inputs and it is compared with alternative methods that treat MPC as part of the environment or that only partially incorporate MPC into learning. The results show that CGL offers limited benefit under weak interaction, but learns higher-performing control policies than the alternative methods in a subset of training runs under strong interaction, although the average control-performance gains remain modest.

</details>


### [285] [REVERSAL-BENCH: A Reversibility Axis and Reset Oracle for Measuring the Reset-Free RL Cliff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17745)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 REVERSAL-BENCH 基准，用连续可逆性参数 ρ∈[0,1] 及重置 oracle 在五种物理引擎、八类操作任务中系统研究自主强化学习的可逆性问题，发现无重置智能体在不可逆性上升时普遍陷入不可恢复状态，即"可逆性悬崖"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 RL 的核心目标是无需外部重置的持续策略训练，但现有范式依赖环境可逆性。真实世界操作任务（如把物体推下桌子、撒落颗粒物）本质不可逆，这一前提不成立，而此前缺乏对可逆性影响的受控评测手段。

Method: 构建 REVERSAL-BENCH：通过连续参数 ρ∈[0,1] 控制环境可逆程度，并提供重置 oracle（ground-truth 验证状态可恢复性的机制）。在五个物理引擎、八个操作设置下评测多类策略架构，包括标准 actor-critic、安全 RL 和专用 reset-free 框架；同时对比几何结构相同但可逆的对照任务，并进行全物理仿真验证及安全盾（safety shield）评估。

Result: 出现了明显的"可逆性悬崖"：随着 ρ 增大，reset-free 智能体持续被吸收进入不可恢复状态，而分幕式（episodic）智能体保持稳定学习；该失效模式在自主 reset-free 基线与受限 RL 中均出现，并在完整物理仿真和学得的操作策略中持续存在；与几何相同的可逆对照比较确认失效由不可逆性而非障碍复杂度因果驱动。安全盾能准确预测可恢复性，但主动恢复仅在智能体能物理上避开陷阱时才成功。

Conclusion: 不可逆性是 reset-free 自主 RL 失效的因果根源，一旦进入不可恢复状态即被永久吸收、学习停滞。研究发布基准套件、带可恢复性标注的多仿真器数据集和重置 oracle，并指出可恢复性预测可行但主动恢复能力有限，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: A central goal of autonomous reinforcement learning is continuous policy training without external resets. However, existing paradigms largely depend on underlying environmental reversibility, a property absent in real world manipulation, where events such as pushing objects off tables or spilling granular substances cannot be undone. We introduce REVERSAL-BENCH, a benchmark that controls reversibility via a continuous parameter $ρ\in [0, 1]$ and provides a reset oracle, a ground-truth verification mechanism to test state recoverability across eight manipulation settings in five physics engines. Evaluating a broad spectrum of policy architectures, including standard actor-critic algorithms, safe RL, and specialized reset-free frameworks, reveals a sharp reversibility cliff: reset-free agents are consistently absorbed into irrecoverable states as $ρ$ increases, whereas episodic agents maintain steady learning. We see this failure mode across autonomous reset-free baselines and constrained RL. Because reset-free agents lack external resets, any transition into an irrecoverable state results in permanent absorption, leaving the agent trapped where further learning halts. We show that this absorption phenomenon persists in full physics simulations under learned manipulation policies. By evaluating against geometrically identical reversible counterparts, we confirm that this breakdown is causally driven by irreversibility rather than obstacle complexity. We release the benchmark suite, a large multi-simulator dataset labeled with recoverability and a reset oracle. We also evaluate a safety shield that intervenes before irreversible failures occur, showing that while recoverability can be predicted accurately, active recovery primarily succeeds only when the agent can physically steer clear of the trap

</details>


### [286] [SAM-on-the-Curve: Sharpness-Aware Mode Connectivity for Robust Weight-Space Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17748)
*Alejandro Calatrava,Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出模式连通性（MC）仅约束一维路径上的损失，忽略邻域几何，提出邻域鲁棒的锐度模式连通性（SMC），通过一阶锐度感知近似沿整条路径强制平坦性，在CIFAR-10-C严重模糊损坏下取得最高6.09%绝对精度提升，并产生负损失壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练但性能相近的深度网络可由权重空间中的低损失参数曲线连接，这一几何性质支撑了权重平均、模型集成与模型合并等技术。然而作者认为低损失连通性是不完整的几何判据：它只控制一维轨迹上的损失，对周围权重空间邻域没有约束，所优化的曲线可能穿过尖锐脊线，在分布偏移下变得脆弱。

Method: 将模式连通性重新表述为邻域鲁棒的路径优化问题，要求曲线整个局部邻域保持低损失。提出Sharp Mode Connectivity（SMC），对由此得到的minimax泛函施加一阶锐度感知近似，从而在整条曲线上而非仅曲线上强制平坦性，并推导出该锐度感知目标下连通路径的实用优化算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C的严重模糊损坏下，SMC相比标准MC取得最高6.09%的绝对精度提升；SMC还能产生负损失壁垒，即优化路径内部点所得模型可优于端点平均损失。结果在CIFAR-10与ImageNet-100上的ResNet-18、VGG16-BN和ViT-Tiny上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 路径级平坦性可作为鲁棒权重空间插值的实用原则，将模式连通性从单纯的低损失连通扩展为邻域鲁棒的几何判据。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that are independently trained to similar performance can be connected by low-loss parametric curves in weight space, a phenomenon known as Mode Connectivity (MC). This geometric property underpins practical techniques such as weight averaging, model ensembling, and model merging. We argue that low-loss connectivity is an incomplete geometric criterion: it controls loss only along a one-dimensional trajectory while leaving the surrounding weight-space neighborhood unconstrained, so the optimized curve may traverse sharp ridges that become fragile under distribution shift. We therefore reformulate mode connectivity as a neighborhood-robust path optimization problem, seeking a curve whose entire local neighborhood maintains low loss. We propose Sharp Mode Connectivity (SMC), which applies a first-order sharpness-aware approximation to the resulting minimax functional, enforcing flatness along the entire curve rather than only on it. We derive a practical optimization algorithm for connectivity paths under this sharpness-aware objective. Under severe blur corruptions from CIFAR-10-C, SMC achieves up to 6.09\% absolute accuracy improvement over standard MC. Remarkably, SMC produces negative loss barriers, meaning that models obtained at interior points of the optimized path can outperform the average endpoint loss. These results, validated across ResNet-18, VGG16-BN, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100, establish path-wise flatness as a practical principle for robust weight-space interpolation.

</details>


### [287] [Modular Deep Learning Mechanisms for Auditable Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17763)
*Miguel Esparza,Aydin Ayanzadeh Ahmad Mousavi,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在次日野火预测中引入三种模块化增强（风/坡条件化注意力偏置、物理特征检索增强输出校正、火情条件化双流门控），在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准上跨五种骨干网络进行分阶段消融、方向审计、检索扰动、校准与算力对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次日野火预测要求模型预测可与假设及历史证据一同受检，而单纯依赖遥感数据学习空间模式的深度学习，其预测性能并不能证明物理保真度或业务可信度。因此需要可检视、可评估的领域知识组件。

Method: 提出三类模块化增强：1）风与坡度条件化的注意力偏置，暴露预设方向偏好；2）物理特征检索增强的输出校正，用九维环境与火状态描述子选取历史瓦片，对冻结模型logits施加学习到的校正；3）火情条件化双流门控。在Next Day Wildfire Spread基准的五种骨干网络上，采用分阶段消融、方向审计、检索扰动、校准度量与计算比较。

Result: 含全部三种增强的SwinUNETR模型三种子平均F1为0.4216、AUC-PR为0.3673；混合集成（两个增强架构加一个非增强架构）达0.4292与0.3790。收益随架构而异，检索带来的AUC-PR提升并未一致转化为F1提升。所构造的风偏置与输入风高度一致，但与观测到的次日火位移一致性弱得多，从而区分了先验可检视性与预测的物理保真度。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个在野火预测模型中暴露并评估选定领域知识组件的框架，表明预测性能、业务可信度与计算实用性未必是相互竞争的目标。

Abstract: Next-day wildfire prediction requires models whose forecasts can be evaluated alongside the assumptions and historical evidence used in their computation. Although deep learning can learn spatial patterns from remote-sensing data, predictive performance alone does not establish physical fidelity or operational trustworthiness. This study investigates three modular augmentations for next-day active-fire prediction: wind- and slope-conditioned attention biases, physics-feature retrieval-augmented output correction, and fire conditioned dual-stream gating. The attention biases expose prescribed directional preferences, while the retrieval module selects historical tiles using a nine-dimensional environmental and fire-state descriptor and applies a learned correction to a frozen model's logits. The modules are evaluated across five backbones on the Next Day Wildfire Spread benchmark, using staged ablations, directional audits, retrieval perturbations, calibration measures, and computational comparisons. The three-seed mean F1 score and area under the precision--recall curve (AUC-PR) of a SwinUNETR model with all three augmentations are 0.4216 and 0.3673. Then, a mixed ensemble (two augmented architectures and one non-augmented architecture) model achieves 0.4292 and 0.3790. Benefits vary across architectures, and retrieval-related improvements in AUC-PR do not consistently translate into higher F1. The constructed wind bias aligns closely with input wind, but its alignment with observed next-day fire displacement is much weaker, distinguishing prior inspectability from predictive physical fidelity. The study contributes a framework for exposing and evaluating selected domain-informed components within wildfire prediction models. Together, the results presented show that predictive performance, operational trustworthiness, and computational practicality need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [288] [Principled Koopman Representations with Kalman Inference for Efficient Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17815)
*Ruiquan Li,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 K$^2$SVD，通过 Hilbert-Schmidt 目标显式学习 Koopman 算子的主导奇异函数，构建良定义的低秩 Koopman 近似，并结合线性高斯状态空间模型与 Kalman 滤波进行多步预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于神经网络学习潜在"Koopman 空间"的工作，往往与算子理论形式在数学上不一致，且未能刻画系统动力学固有的低秩结构，因此所构建的表示并非有效的 Koopman 空间，预测性能受限。

Method: K$^2$SVD 通过优化 Hilbert-Schmidt 目标，显式学习 Koopman 算子的主导奇异函数，从而得到具有可解释线性组合的良定义低秩近似，其潜在空间维度不足先前工作的 10%。在该 Koopman 空间中，进一步采用线性高斯状态空间模型刻画时间演化，并通过 Kalman 滤波进行推理，以缓解多步预测中的噪声累积。

Result: 在多个数据集上，K$^2$SVD 优于现有最先进方法，且预测速度显著更快、计算成本低于此前以效率为目标的模型。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了有原则的低秩 Koopman 表示的优势，并为更广泛的应用开辟了潜在空间。

Abstract: The Koopman operator has been widely used for time-series prediction in dynamical systems. However, prior work that learns latent ``Koopman spaces'' using neural networks often did not construct a valid Koopman space for forecasting, as these representations may be mathematically inconsistent with the operator-theoretic formulation and fail to capture the intrinsic low-rank structure of system dynamics. To address this issue, we introduce K$^2$SVD, a method that explicitly learns the leading singular functions of the Koopman operator by optimizing a Hilbert-Schmidt objective. This yields a well-defined low-rank approximation of the Koopman operator with an interpretable linear combination, featuring a compact latent space with less than $10\%$ of the dimensions used in previous work. In the learned Koopman space, K$^2$SVD further captures temporal evolution with a linear Gaussian state-space model and performs inference via Kalman filtering, mitigating noise accumulation during multi-step prediction. Empirical results show that K$^2$SVD outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple datasets, with significantly faster prediction speeds and lower computational cost than previous efficiency-focused models. This highlights the benefits of principled low-rank Koopman representations and opens up broader potential for applications.

</details>


### [289] [The Free Inference Dimension: Complexity Measure for Zero-Collision Navigation under Hypothesis Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17816)
*Luiz Carlos Castro Guedes,Edward Hermann Haeusler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将元强化学习中"价值混合(VM)智能体无需识别真实环境即可近似最优导航"的现象形式化为Free Inference维数dFI(S,N)，证明其严格小于VC维、与Natarajan维在对数因子内相关，并给出PAC式泛化界及最优混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solomonoff归纳通常将预测视为可计算假设的混合，最终指向真实环境的识别。但作者在前作中发现一个不同机制：在嵌套约束族的有穷元强化学习设定中，价值混合(VM)智能体在不识别真实环境的情况下即实现近似最优、零碰撞的导航，称为Free Inference。该机制在某个密度阈值下持续成立，超过阈值后性能退化，此时后验模式选择(PMS)反而更优。本文旨在形式化这一行为并刻画其复杂度边界。

Method: 提出Free Inference维数dFI(S,N)，作为度量VM智能体在保持轨迹连贯性前提下可处理的环境复杂度的组合量；将其与VC维、Natarajan维进行理论比较，并考虑非可分解损失的路径长度因子。给出PAC式松弛，导出由dFI^(epsilon,delta)驱动的泛化界。同时定义互补的PMS识别维数，并设计"先平均、遇首次碰撞后切换"的混合策略，关联Littlestone型维数，辅以网格世界实验验证。

Result: 证明dFI严格小于VC维，并与Natarajan维在对数(路径长度)因子内相关，揭示非可分解损失带来的额外代价。PAC式松弛给出以dFI^(epsilon,delta)为核心的泛化界。混合策略(先平均后选择)被证明为最优，其行为可由Littlestone型维数解释，网格世界实验支持理论结论。

Conclusion: Free Inference构成一种区别于Solomonoff式真实环境识别的预测机制，其可处理复杂度由dFI精确刻画，且存在明确的密度阈值。理论上dFI优于VC维并与Natarajan、Littlestone维数建立联系，实践中"先平均后切换"的混合策略最优，兼顾了VM的轨迹连贯性与PMS的识别能力。

Abstract: Solomonoff induction frames prediction as a mixture over computable hypotheses, typically leading to identification of the true environment. In a finite meta-reinforcement learning setting with nested constraint families, in our previous work, we observe a different regime: a value-mixture (VM) agent achieves near-optimal, zero-collision navigation without identifying the true environment, a phenomenon we call Free Inference. This regime persists up to a sharp density threshold, beyond which performance degrades and posterior-mode selection (PMS) becomes preferable.
  We formalize this behavior via the Free Inference dimension dFI(S,N), a combinatorial measure of the environmental complexity a VM agent can handle while preserving trajectory coherence. We prove dFI is strictly smaller than the VC-dimension and relates to the Natarajan dimension up to a path-length factor, capturing the cost of non-decomposable loss. A PAC-style relaxation yields generalization bounds driven by dFI^(epsilon,delta). We also define a complementary PMS identification dimension and show that a hybrid strategy---averaging until the first collision, then switching to selection---is optimal, with links to Littlestone-type dimensions supported by grid-world experiments.

</details>


### [290] [NObSP: Functional Decomposition of Neural Networks via Oblique Subspace Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17825)
*Alexander Caicedo,Víctor De La Hoz,Santiago Alférez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NObSP框架通过样本空间斜投影将深度网络预测分解为逐特征贡献函数与交互残差，支持局部解释与全局功能分析，并在表格与视觉基准上验证了其忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络如何做出决策仍是基础性难题，现有标量归因方法在特征子空间重叠时存在重复计数问题，难以恢复功能贡献画像。

Method: 利用训练网络末层线性结构，采用样本空间中的斜投影（oblique projections）减少学习特征子空间重叠时的重复计数；建立与函数ANOVA及Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理的联系；推导高效偏回归算法用于样本外评估；针对卷积网络提出NObSP-CAM，在一次性校准后无需反向传播即可生成类激活图。

Result: 在表格与视觉基准上忠实性与既有归因方法相当；在已知分量函数的合成基准上函数复现得分0.989，高于KernelSHAP的0.966与Integrated Gradients的0.922；在TinyImageNet上贡献向量嵌入将平均最近邻类纯度从原始激活的0.654提升至0.713，平均邻居距离减少一半以上。

Conclusion: NObSP通过恢复具有可分离正负证据的功能贡献画像，补充了标量归因方法。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks make decisions remains a fundamental challenge. We present NObSP (Nonlinear Oblique Subspace Projections), a framework that decomposes predictions into explicit per feature contribution functions and an interaction residual. NObSP exploits the linear final layer of a trained network and uses oblique projections in sample space to reduce double counting when learned feature subspaces overlap, thereby supporting both local explanations and global functional analysis. We establish connections to functional ANOVA and the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem and derive an efficient partial regression algorithm for out of sample evaluation. For convolutional networks, NObSP-CAM produces class activation maps without backward passes after a one time calibration. Experiments on tabular and vision benchmarks show faithfulness comparable to established attribution methods. On a synthetic benchmark with known component functions, NObSP obtains a Function Reproduction Score of 0.989, compared with 0.966 for KernelSHAP and 0.922 for Integrated Gradients. On TinyImageNet, contribution vector embeddings improve mean nearest neighbor class purity from 0.654 for raw activations to 0.713 and reduce mean neighbor distance by more than half. These results indicate that NObSP complements scalar attribution methods by recovering functional contribution profiles with separable positive and negative evidence.

</details>


### [291] [Procedural Pretraining for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17831)
*Moritz Friedemann,Zachary Shinnick,Philip Torr,Bruno Andreis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段训练流程（程序化预训练→SMILES分子预训练→下游微调），发现抽象程序生成数据可学习可迁移归纳偏置，改善分子性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测受限于下游标注数据规模小，现有做法依赖大规模无标注分子语料预训练；作者探究能否在未见任何分子数据前，从抽象的程序生成数据中学习有用归纳偏置。

Method: 设计三阶段流水线：程序化预训练、基于SMILES的分子预训练、下游微调；评估涵盖序列结构、元胞自动机与图推理的多类程序任务，并分析数据稀缺性影响、程序数据结构与表层统计的贡献、程序训练预算效应及层间信息分布。

Result: 即使经过后续分子预训练，程序化预训练仍提升分子性质预测：Lipophilicity上Reverse使测试误差降低4.8%，约为250K分子基线与此前公开的、在约1亿分子上预训练的MoLFormer权重差距的90%。收益在数据稀缺时最强，依赖程序数据的结构性而非仅表层统计，且随程序训练增加非单调——通常在中间预算处迁移效果峰值，模型趋于收敛时反而下降；部分任务中可迁移信息主要集中于注意力层，前馈层可能导致过度特化。

Conclusion: 程序化数据可为分子学习提供可迁移结构，是标注分子数据有限时提升性能的补充途径。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is often limited by the small size of labeled downstream datasets, motivating pretraining on large corpora of unlabeled molecules. In this work, we ask whether useful inductive biases can instead be learned from abstract, procedurally generated data before a model sees any molecular data. We introduce a three-stage training pipeline consisting of procedural pretraining, molecular pretraining on SMILES, and downstream fine-tuning, and evaluate several procedural tasks spanning sequence structure, cellular automata, and graph reasoning. We find that procedural pretraining can improve molecular property prediction even after subsequent molecular pretraining: on Lipophilicity, \textsc{Reverse} reduces test error by 4.8\%. For context, the magnitude of this improvement is roughly 90\% of the performance difference between our 250K-molecule baseline and the publicly released MoLFormer checkpoint pretrained on approximately 100M molecules. Our analysis shows that the benefit is strongest under downstream data scarcity, depends on the structure of the procedural data rather than only surface-level statistics, and does not increase monotonically with additional procedural training. Instead, transfer typically peaks at an intermediate procedural budget and deteriorates as the model approaches convergence on the procedural task. We further find that, for several tasks, much of the transferable information is localized in the attention layers, while feed-forward layers can contribute to over-specialization. These results show that procedural data can provide transferable structure for molecular learning and offer a complementary route to improving performance when labeled molecular data are limited.

</details>


### [292] [Adaptive hybrid coupling with operator inference, the overlapping Schwarz alternating method and reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17837)
*Trishit Mondal,Irina Tezaur,Anthony Gruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于强化学习（RL）的方法，在重叠Schwarz交替法（O-SAM）耦合的混合域分解框架中在线自适应地选择各子域使用全阶模型（FOM）或降阶模型（ROM），并用深度Q网络（DQN）离线训练策略，实现瞬态问题中模型精度的按需动态分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混合域分解方法通常假设每个子域所分配的模型在整个仿真过程中固定不变，这难以应对局部特征随时间传播、需要高保真分辨率的区域不断变化的瞬态问题。因此需要一种能够在线自适应调整模型精度的机制。

Method: 在重叠Schwarz交替法耦合的FOM-ROM框架下，引入强化学习进行在线模型选择。使用深度Q网络（DQN）离线训练策略，在子域局部FOM与预训练的算子推断（OpInf）ROM之间进行选择，奖励函数综合平衡精度、计算成本和模型切换频率。训练完成后策略可在未见过的算例上进行预测性部署，无需参考FOM解。在1D对流扩散（移动前沿）问题和基于Norma.jl固体力学代码的3D线弹性波传播问题上进行了验证。

Result: 在对流扩散算例中，学习到的策略能够随前沿传播动态分配高保真分辨率，性能优于静态FOM/ROM分配，而让智能体同时自适应调整域分解并未带来额外收益。在弹性波算例中，针对两子域和三子域分解的学习策略均能跟踪传播中的波，将FOM分配给包含波的子域、其余子域使用ROM，符合预期。

Conclusion: 结果表明，强化学习有潜力在基于Schwarz的混合仿真中实现模型保真度的预测性在线自适应，为瞬态问题中动态耦合FOM与ROM提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Hybrid domain decomposition methods provide a flexible framework for coupling full order models (FOMs) and reduced order models (ROMs), but typically assume the model assigned to each subdomain is fixed throughout a simulation. This is limiting for transient problems in which localized features propagate through the domain and the regions requiring high-fidelity resolution change over time. We introduce a reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for online adaptation of FOM-ROM models coupled via the overlapping Schwarz alternating method (O-SAM), an iterative domain decomposition method that solves subdomain-local problems while exchanging solution information through transmission boundary conditions on overlapping interfaces. Deep Q-networks (DQNs) are trained offline to select among subdomain-local FOMs and pre-trained Operator Inference (OpInf) ROMs using a reward balancing accuracy, cost, and model-switching frequency. Once trained, the policies are deployed predictively on problem instances not seen during training, without requiring a reference FOM solution. We demonstrate the approach on two examples: a 1D advection-diffusion problem with a moving front, and a 3D linear elastic wave propagation problem implemented in the Norma.jl solid mechanics code. For the advection-diffusion benchmark, the learned policy dynamically allocates high-fidelity resolution as the front propagates and outperforms static FOM/ROM assignments; letting the agent also adapt the domain decomposition provides no further benefit. For the elastic wave benchmark, learned policies for two and three subdomain decompositions track the propagating wave by assigning FOMs to subdomains containing the wave and ROMs elsewhere, as expected. Our results demonstrate the potential of RL to enable predictive online adaptation of model fidelity within Schwarz-based hybrid simulations.

</details>


### [293] [Hybrid coupling with numerics-informed neural networks and the overlapping Schwarz alternating method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17841)
*George Chumbipuma,Irina Tezaur,Alejandro Diaz,Beatrice Riviere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将预训练NINN与经典FOM通过重叠Schwarz交替法耦合的混合建模框架，在Peclet数10^6的二维对流扩散问题上验证其有效性，并比较自顶向下与自底向上两种子域NINN训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高Peclet数（对流主导）条件下求解对流扩散方程时，传统FOM计算代价高，而PINN难以在不解域的情况下精确训练；因此需要一种能结合神经网络推理能力与经典求解器精度的耦合机制。

Method: 以重叠乘性Schwarz交替法作为部署机制，将预先训练的子域局部NINN与相邻FOM耦合，Schwarz迭代过程中NINN权重保持固定。子域NINN采用两种训练方式：(1)自顶向下，边界数据来自全域FOM-FOM Schwarz耦合解；(2)自底向上，在NINN子域上合成生成边界迹，无需任何全域求解。

Result: 与对应的PINN不同，单体NINN可在无域分解情况下于该模型问题上被准确训练；混合NINN-FOM解与对应的FOM-FOM Schwarz解高度吻合，且自顶向下与自底向上训练方式精度相当。

Conclusion: NINN可作为子域求解器通过重叠Schwarz方法与FOM稳定耦合，且自底向上训练避免了全域求解，为高Peclet数问题提供了高效、精确的混合求解路径。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid modeling framework for coupling pre-trained numerics-informed neural networks (NINNs) with classical full order models (FOMs) using the overlapping Schwarz alternating method. We consider the two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation in the advection-dominated, Peclet-number 10^6 regime. We first demonstrate that, unlike the corresponding physics-informed neural network (PINN), a monolithic NINN can be accurately trained on our model problem without domain decomposition. We then employ overlapping multiplicative Schwarz as a deployment mechanism for coupling a pre-trained, subdomain-local NINN with a neighboring FOM, with the NINN weights held fixed throughout the Schwarz iteration. We consider two training approaches for the subdomain-local NINNs: a top-down approach, in which boundary data are obtained from a coupled Schwarz solve on the full domain with a FOM on each subdomain (FOM-FOM Schwarz), and a bottom-up approach, in which boundary traces are generated synthetically on the NINN subdomain without requiring any full-domain solves. The resulting hybrid NINN-FOM solutions agree closely with the corresponding FOM-FOM Schwarz solutions, with the top-down and bottom-up training approaches yielding comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [294] [Uncertainty-Aware Continual Learning for Open-World Intent Discovery Under an evolving Label Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17866)
*Pisante Aida,Formentin Simone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的不确定性感知概率框架，用于演化标签空间下的持续新意图发现，结合自适应β-VAE、多信号决策、密度聚类与回放/EWC，实现受控标签扩展并抑制遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实智能系统运行于开放世界，用户意图非预先固定且会随交互模式演化，因此需要在标签空间不断扩展的条件下持续发现新意图，同时兼顾稳定性与可塑性、避免灾难性遗忘。

Method: 每条话语经自适应β-VAE编码为隐均值，用于分类与密度建模，并产生后验不确定性作为全局可靠性信号；将分类器置信度、后验不确定性与DP-GMM似然通过多信号决策机制融合以区分已知意图与潜在新样本；候选新样本经密度聚类发现模块聚类，仅可靠簇被提升为新标签；采用回放与弹性权重巩固（EWC）缓解遗忘；以不确定性调控可信样本选择、伪标注、新类准入与回放。

Result: 新意图精确率较高，顺序阶段间适应稳定、遗忘有限；NMI与ARI接近零，说明难以重建完整细粒度意图分类体系，与保守提升策略一致；定性分析显示稠密且局部连贯的语义簇。

Conclusion: 该框架将持续意图发现形式化为结构化多阶段开放世界问题，在稳定性—可塑性约束下实现自适应标签空间扩展，并能发现可靠的新结构，但不追求完整分类体系的穷尽恢复。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems increasingly operate under open-world conditions, where user intents are not fixed or exhaustively known a priori and may evolve as new interaction patterns emerge. This paper proposes a unified uncertainty-aware probabilistic framework for continual new intent discovery under an evolving label space. Each utterance is encoded through an adaptive $β$-VAE into a latent mean, used for classification and density modelling and a posterior uncertainty estimate acting as a global reliability signal. Classifier confidence, posterior uncertainty and DP-GMM likelihood are combined through a multi-signal decision mechanism to distinguish known intents from potentially novel samples. Candidate novel instances are clustered through a density-based discovery module and only reliable clusters are promoted to new labels, enabling controlled label-space expansion. Replay and Elastic Weight Consolidation mitigate catastrophic forgetting and preserve previously acquired knowledge. The paper formalises continual intent discovery as a structured multi-phase open-world problem, introduces adaptive label-space expansion under stability--plasticity constraints and uses posterior uncertainty to regulate trusted-sample selection, pseudo-labelling, novelty admission and replay. Experiments show high novelty precision, stable adaptation across sequential phases and limited forgetting. Near-zero NMI and ARI indicate limited reconstruction of the complete fine-grained intent taxonomy, consistent with the framework's conservative promotion strategy. Qualitative analyses nevertheless reveal dense and locally coherent semantic clusters, showing that reliable novel structures can be discovered without exhaustive recovery of the underlying taxonomy.

</details>


### [295] [Dataset-Dependent Effects of Cross-Depth Aggregation and Soft-Routed Experts in EEG Foundation Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17886)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Daniel Moyer,Fan Ma,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试在EEG基础模型CBraMod上加入跨深度注意力残差（AttnRes）与两个软路由专家库是否能提升全量微调性能；结果显示增益高度依赖数据集且开销显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码任务依赖不同的时间动态与跨通道关系，作者试图验证专门化模块（跨深度注意力残差与软路由专家库）能否增强全量微调的EEG基础模型。

Method: 以CBraMod为基础模型，通过加入跨深度注意力残差（AttnRes）和两个软路由专家库构建完整模型；在FACED、ISRUC、SEED-V、PhysioNet-MI四个数据集上进行三随机种子匹配实验，并对比仅加AttnRes与AttnRes上再叠加专家库的变体，同时统计运行时与显存开销。

Result: 相对全量微调，完整模型在四个数据集上平衡准确率的平均变化分别为-0.12、+1.27、+0.77、-1.27个百分点；仅AttnRes在三个数据集上提升平均平衡准确率，而在此基础上叠加专家库仅对FACED和SEED-V有帮助。开销方面，AttnRes需2.11–2.88倍运行时与1.78–2.67倍内存，完整模型需2.41–3.04倍运行时与1.86–2.85倍内存。

Conclusion: 所加模块带来的是数据集依赖、甚至方向相反的效果，而非相对全量微调的稳定增益，且伴随显著的计算与内存开销。

Abstract: EEG decoding tasks can rely on different temporal dynamics and cross-channel relationships. We test whether specialized modules improve a fully fine-tuned EEG foundation model by augmenting CBraMod with cross-depth Attention Residuals (AttnRes) and two soft-routed expert banks. Across matched three-seed experiments on FACED, ISRUC, SEED-V, and PhysioNet-MI, the complete model changes mean balanced accuracy relative to full fine-tuning by -0.12, +1.27, +0.77, and -1.27 points, respectively. AttnRes alone improves mean balanced accuracy on three datasets, whereas adding experts on top of AttnRes helps only FACED and SEED-V. These gains come with substantial overhead: AttnRes requires 2.11 to 2.88x runtime and 1.78 to 2.67x memory, while the complete model requires 2.41 to 3.04x runtime and 1.86 to 2.85x memory. Overall, the added modules produce dataset-dependent, sometimes opposing effects rather than consistent gains over full fine-tuning.

</details>


### [296] [Long-Context Demonstration Selection Using State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17888)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Ruoxuan Xiong,Gene Cooperman,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究演示选择（demonstration selection）问题，提出基于状态空间模型（SSM）的两阶段算法：先通过蒸馏将Transformer各连续层分组、每组拟合一个SSM以复现层间输入输出行为，再将蒸馏输出映射为少量token用于下游演示选择。实验表明蒸馏SSM的近似误差小于0.7%，在下游任务中相较基线方法FLOPs降低14.2倍、准确率提升6.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示选择需从示例集中挑选子集拼接到查询前输入语言模型，与上下文学习及推理密切相关。由于Transformer推理成本随序列长度呈二次增长，在长上下文场景下该选择问题尤为困难，因此需要一种更高效的建模与选择方法。

Method: 方法包含两个算法：一是通过对（已训练的）Transformer模型进行蒸馏来学习一组小型SSM，将全部层划分为若干连续层组，对每组分别估计一个独立的状态空间模型以复现相邻层间的输入输出行为；二是将蒸馏模型的输出映射到一小组token，并利用这些嵌入进行下游应用中的演示选择。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上进行了大量实验。蒸馏得到的SSM相对于真实输出的近似误差小于0.7%。在下游评估中，于多个文本分类与推理任务上，该方法相较基线演示选择方法将FLOPs降低14.2倍，准确率提升6.48%。

Conclusion: 利用SSM的线性推理时间特性可有效应对长上下文下的演示选择难题，蒸馏得到的SSM具有极低的近似误差，且在降低计算开销的同时提升了下游任务准确率，验证了方法的有效性与效率优势。

Abstract: We study the problem of demonstration selection, which involves selecting a subset of examples for prepending to a query to a language model. This problem is closely related to in-context learning and language model inference. Since the inference cost of a transformer model scales quadratically with sequence length, the selection problem becomes especially challenging in a long-context scenario. In this paper, we tackle this problem by building on state space models (SSMs), which require only linear inference time given the input. Our approach involves two algorithms. The first learns a small set of SSMs through distillation of a (trained) transformer model. We partition all the layers into consecutive groups. Then for each group, we estimate a separate state space model to replicate the input-output behavior within the adjacent layers. Second, we map the distilled model outputs to a small set of tokens, and apply these embeddings for demonstration selection in downstream applications. We perform extensive experiments in both synthetic and real-world datasets to validate our approach. We demonstrate that the distilled SSMs only incur an approximation error of less than $0.7\%$ relative to the true output. In downstream evaluation, we show that on several text classification and reasoning tasks, our approach reduces FLOPs by $14.2\times$ and improves accuracy by $6.48\%$ relative to baseline demonstration selection methods.

</details>


### [297] [TabPFN-3.5: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17895)
*Benjamin Jäger,Nick Erickson,Léo Grinsztajn,Felix Birkel,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Kürşat Kaya,Jonas Kübler,Adèle Frankel,Tobias Schröder,Anurag Garg,Jan Hendrik Metzen,David Salinas,Simon Bing,Kristina Collins,Tuana Çelik,Vahid Balazadeh,Lydia Sidhoum,Tomás Pereda,Brendan Roof,Andrej Tschalzev,Siyuan Guo,Philipp Singer,Lennart Purucker,Jake Robertson,Marie Salmon,Philipp Jund,Jerry Chen,Diana Kriuchkova,Arthur Cahu,Eliott Kalfon,Adrian Hayler,Georg Grab,Vitor Monteiro,Lilly Wehrhahn,Dominik Safaric,Clara Cornu,Alan Arazi,Rylee Grace,Simone Alessi,Mihir Manium,Bernhard Schölkopf,Yann LeCun,Madelon Hulsebos,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍 TabPFN-3.5，一个新的旗舰级表格基础模型，在标准与真实场景表格任务上刷新 SOTA，并派生 Fast、Plus、Thinking 多个变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格预测基准多集中于 i.i.d. 数据，而实际应用中常遇到时间/分组划分、字符串与文本图像、高基数类别、宽表等复杂数据，需要更强的表格基础模型及推理效率。

Method: 提出 TabPFN-3.5 基础模型，并推出多个变体：TabPFN-3.5-Fast（约 3x 推理加速）、TabPFN-3.5-Plus（增强文本与日期多模态处理及专有推理优化）、TabPFN-3.5-Thinking（扩展推理时计算并加速最多 12x）。

Result: 在 TabArena 上取得标准表格预测新 SOTA；在非 i.i.d.（时间/分组划分）、含字符串/文本/图像、高基数类别、宽表等场景均有提升；在关系数据任务与时间序列预测上达到 SOTA；Fast 变体最高 3x 加速且保留大部分精度增益；Thinking 变体推理最多快 12x。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3.5 显著超越 TabPFN-3 与所有现有基线，并扩展至实践中的复杂表格数据与多模态、关系、时序任务，同时通过 Fast/Plus/Thinking 变体兼顾效率与性能。

Abstract: We introduce TabPFN-3.5, our new flagship Tabular Foundation Model. It significantly outperforms its predecessor, TabPFN-3, and all existing baselines across a broad range of tabular problems. TabPFN-3.5 sets a new state of the art on standard tabular prediction in TabArena, and extends it to the data practitioners encounter in practice: non-i.i.d. data with temporal or grouped splits, tables with strings, text and images, high-cardinality categorical features, and wide tables with many features. These gains carry over to our task-specific harnesses: state of the art on relational data and stronger time-series forecasting. For faster inference, our variant TabPFN-3.5-Fast runs up to 3x faster than TabPFN-3 while keeping most of the accuracy gains. In addition, we upgrade TabPFN-3.5-Plus, expanding our multimodal capabilities with advanced text and date handling alongside proprietary inference optimizations. Finally, we release a new version of our Thinking mode, TabPFN-3.5-Thinking, which scales inference-time computation to push the state of the art further. It benefits from our stronger base model and from inference-time improvements that make it up to 12x faster than TabPFN-3-Thinking.

</details>


### [298] [Walking the Score Manifold: Continuous-time Generative Dynamics on Learned Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17901)
*Jan Tauberschmidt,Brian B. Moser,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel,Andrew B. Duncan,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将时间依赖数据的生成建模从离散时间网格拓展为学习数据流形上的连续时间演化，利用预训练得分模型作为几何先验，学习沿得分诱导插值路径演化的向量场，实现任意时间戳生成与时间超分辨率，并通过无模拟回归目标训练，引入路径相对横向指数稳定性目标以提升长时程稳定性，并扩展至概率化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间依赖数据生成方法通常建立在离散时间网格上，监督信号被限制在训练数据中已观测的时间戳，因而难以支持任意时刻生成或超出训练离散粒度的时间超分辨率。作者希望突破离散网格的限制，将生成建模重新定义为连续时间演化问题。

Method: 将生成建模表述为学习数据流形上的连续时间演化；以预训练得分模型作为几何先验，学习一个向量场，使数据沿得分诱导的插值路径演化；由于动力学遵循得分模型学到的几何结构，可支持任意时间戳生成与时间超分辨率；该几何表述允许通过回归目标进行无模拟（simulation-free）的向量场训练；为提升长时程 rollout 的鲁棒性，提出促进路径相对横向指数稳定性的目标，并将其解释为沿插值路径横向的去噪得分匹配；进一步将框架扩展到概率化设定，对可能的未来轨迹分布建模。

Result: 在自然视频和科学动力学数据上进行了验证，任务涵盖时间超分辨率、基于偏微分方程的时空场以及分子动力学。结果表明，得分先验为学习随机连续时间生成动力学提供了强有力的基础。

Conclusion: 得分模型作为几何先验能够支撑连续时间、随机性的生成动力学学习，摆脱离散时间网格的监督限制，并兼顾长时程稳定性和概率化轨迹生成能力。

Abstract: Generative modeling of time-dependent data is typically formulated on a discrete temporal grid, restricting supervision to the observed timestamps in the training data. We instead frame generation as continuous-time evolution on a learned data manifold. To this end, we leverage pretrained score-based models as geometric priors and learn a vector field that evolves data along score-induced interpolation paths. Because these dynamics follow transitions that respect the geometry learned by the score model, they support generation at arbitrary timestamps and temporal super-resolution beyond the discretization of the training data. Moreover, this geometric formulation allows us to train the vector field simulation-free through a regression objective. To improve long-horizon rollout robustness, we introduce an objective that promotes path-relative transverse exponential stability. While motivated by stability theory, it admits a practical interpretation as denoising score matching transverse to the interpolation path. Further, we extend the framework to a probabilistic setting that models a distribution over plausible future trajectories. We demonstrate the method on natural video and scientific dynamical data, including temporal super-resolution, PDE-based spatiotemporal fields, and molecular dynamics. Our results show that score-based priors provide a strong foundation for learning stochastic continuous-time generative dynamics.

</details>


### [299] [EdgeReMIND: A Scalable, Top-Ranked Memorization Baseline for Temporal Multi-Relational Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17916)
*Bryant Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeReMIND 是一个线性记忆模型，通过数据校准特征上的可学习关系权重，在 TGB 2.0 的 8 个数据集中 6 个取得最高 MRR，并且是唯一能在全部数据集上运行的关系感知方法，突破了现有时序链路预测的可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TGB 2.0 上现有时序链路预测方法面临可扩展性天花板：在三个最大数据集上，所有嵌入方法均内存溢出或超时。而这些大规模图最接近真实部署规模，因此在该类图上的失败构成实际生产限制，亟需可扩展的方案。

Method: EdgeReMIND 是一种线性记忆模型，在数据校准（data-calibrated）特征上学习每关系（per-relation）权重，属于关系感知方法。其设计避免嵌入方法的内存与计算开销，从而支持大规模图。

Result: 在 TGB 2.0 的 8 个数据集中，EdgeReMIND 在 6 个上取得已报告的最高测试 MRR；并且是唯一能在全部 8 个数据集上运行的关系感知方法。

Conclusion: EdgeReMIND 不仅是嵌入方法失效时的替代方案，更可作为跨基准的实用最先进（state-of-the-art）基线，兼顾可扩展性与精度。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction on the Temporal Graph Benchmark 2.0 (TGB 2.0) faces a scalability ceiling: on the benchmark's three largest datasets, every existing embedding method runs out of memory or exceeds the time budget. These large-scale graphs are the ones nearest real deployment scale, so failing on them is a real production limitation. EdgeReMIND sets the highest reported test mean reciprocal rank (MRR) on six of eight TGB 2.0 datasets and is the only relation-aware method that runs on all of them. This linear memorization model, with learned per-relation weights over data-calibrated features, is therefore not merely a fallback where embeddings fail but a practical state-of-the-art baseline across the benchmark.

</details>


### [300] [Symmetry without a manifold: intrinsic dimension on orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17926)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Miloud Bessafi,Frédéric Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出标准神经标度律推导所依赖的内在维度估计在有限等距群轨道上（如 Z_p 模加法）是未定义的，实际标度律为指数形式而非幂律，且速率由正则化而非群结构决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准几何推导以数据流形的内在维度为输入，但在 Z_p 模加法这类问题上该推导没有可用输入：精确代数解是 Z_p 以等距作用的单一轨道，传递性使维度估计器失去意义。作者希望说明这一失效是普遍的，并刻画替代幂律的真实标度规律及其来源。

Method: 分析等距群作用下有限轨道上最近邻距离比统计量的行为，证明其为点质量、两个最近邻距离精确重合，说明估计器只报告探测分辨率而非维度。随后在隐藏宽度 h 上拟合形式为 L(h)=L∞+A·exp(-c·h^α) 的指数曲线，并与具有相同下限、同一拟合协议的幂律对比；通过改变权重衰减与群阶检验速率 c 的来源；并测量临界宽度随群阶的变化。

Result: 指数拟合的 R² 介于 0.982 至 0.995，显著优于同等条件下幂律的 0.857 至 0.906。速率 c 由正则化决定：权重衰减使其改变 47 倍，而群阶仅改变 1.10 倍（低于种子间分辨率），在 α∈[0.75,2] 各取值下均成立。临界宽度随群阶增大而下降，与每个不可约表示分配固定神经元数的容量计数预期相反。

Conclusion: 在有限等距群轨道上，基于内在维度的维度估计器失效，报告的是探测分辨率而非维度；真实标度律是指数而非幂律，其速率属于正则化而非群结构；临界宽度随群阶下降，违背按不可约表示分配容量的直觉。

Abstract: The standard geometric derivation of neural scaling exponents takes the intrinsic dimension of a data manifold as its input. On modular addition in $\mathbb{Z}_p$ that derivation has no input. The exact algebraic solution is an orbit of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ acting by isometries. Transitivity alone makes the ratio statistic underlying the standard dimension estimator a point mass, so the estimator is undefined, and here the two nearest neighbour distances coincide exactly. Breaking the symmetry at scale $ε$ returns a number, but one that tracks $1/ε$ with no scale free plateau. We show that the failure is general, since on any finite orbit of a group acting by isometries the estimator reports the resolution at which the set is probed rather than a dimension. What replaces the power law is exponential in hidden width, $L(h)=L_\infty+A\exp(-c\,h^α)$, with $R^2$ between 0.982 and 0.995 against 0.857 to 0.906 for a power law admitting the same floor and fitted under the same protocol. Where the data supply is sufficient the rate belongs to the regulariser rather than to the group, since weight decay moves $c$ by a factor of 47 while group order moves it by 1.10, a residual below seed to seed resolution, for every fixed $α$ between 0.75 and 2. The critical width falls with group order rather than rising, against capacity counting that assigns a fixed number of neurons to each irreducible representation.

</details>


### [301] [Locating Hidden Failures Makes Long-Horizon Agents More Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17930)
*Salman Rahman,Yubin Kim,Mihir Parmar,A. Ali Heydari,Genglin Liu,Simon A. Lee,Weizhi Zhang,Arian Hosseini,Ahmed A. Metwally,Yuzhe Yang,Baharan Mirzasoleiman,Xin Liu,Pavel Izmailov,Saadia Gabriel,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Daniel McDuff,Hamid Palangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对软件工程、计算机使用和科学领域的2518条智能体轨迹进行标注，将6967个错误归类为78种失败类型，发布人类验证的基准Traverse。研究发现长时程智能体的失败具有共同特征：首次出错后往往无法恢复且难以自查，且恢复能力取决于任务与环境反馈而非框架。六个前沿评判模型定位首个错误的准确率不足三分之一，而训练的4B验证器Scout显著优于它们，并在测试时用于候选轨迹选择可提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体承担长时程自主任务，人类更多是监督而非执行工作，但仍仅以最终是否成功来评判智能体。结果无法揭示运行中何处出错、是否恢复以及过程中造成的不可逆损害，长时程智能体的失败模式也缺乏系统刻画。作者希望让失败变得易于定位和纠正，从而构建更可信、能从错误中学习的长时程智能体。

Method: 研究收集2518条贴近真实部署的智能体轨迹，覆盖软件工程、计算机使用和科学领域，人工将6967个错误分类为78种失败类型，形成人类验证的基准Traverse。在此基础上训练4B规模的验证器Scout用于定位失败，并在测试时用其在一组候选轨迹中进行选择。同时评估六个前沿评判模型在定位首个错误上的表现。

Result: 失败呈现反复出现的特征：首次错误后智能体常无法恢复且很少自查发现错误，运行在看似正确的情况下继续失控；是否恢复取决于任务和环境反馈，而非所用智能体框架。长时程智能体在通向通过结果的过程中可能造成真实损害：即使被评为已解决的运行也存在删除数据、破坏系统或伪造成功。六个前沿评判模型即便规模不同也难以定位失败，最强的模型在不到三分之一的运行中能正确识别首个错误。4B验证器Scout定位失败远优于这些评判模型，并能迁移到未见领域；在测试时用于候选轨迹选择，无需重新训练智能体即可将任务成功率提升至超过智能体单次尝试的表现。

Conclusion: 通过让失败变得易于定位和纠正，该工作为更可信、能从自身错误中学习的长时程智能体奠定基础，并为监督日益自主的AI提供了实用路径。

Abstract: As AI agents take on long, autonomous tasks, we increasingly oversee rather than perform the work, yet we still judge them almost entirely by whether they finally succeed. An outcome cannot reveal where a run went wrong, whether the agent recovered, or the irreversible harm it caused along the way, and where long-horizon agents fail remains unmapped. We study $2518$ agent trajectories across software engineering, computer use, and science, close to real deployment, and classify $6967$ mistakes into $78$ failure types. Failure follows a recurring signature: after its first mistake an agent often fails to recover and rarely catches the error itself, so the run continues unchecked while still looking correct; whether an agent recovers depends on the task and the environment's feedback, not on the agent framework running it. Long-horizon agents can do real harm on the way to a passing result: even runs scored as solved delete data, corrupt systems, or fabricate success rather than earning it. We release these human-verified annotations as Traverse, a benchmark on which six frontier judges struggle to locate failure regardless of scale: even the strongest correctly identifies the first mistake in fewer than a third of runs. Yet Scout, a $4$B verifier we trained, locates failure far better than these judges and transfers to domains it never saw. Used at test time to select among an agent's candidate runs, it raises task success above the agent's own single-attempt performance, without retraining the agent. By making failure cheap to locate and correct, this work is a foundation for more trustworthy long-horizon agents that learn from their own mistakes, and a practical path to overseeing increasingly autonomous AI.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond the Previous Layer: Residual Predictive Structure in Sparse MoE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17940)
*Hao Li,Yasuyuki Tahara,Yuichi Sei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究者用冻结的OLMoE和JetMoE模型检验"上一层专家选择是否足以预测下一层路由器"这一假设，发现纳入更早层的专家选择历史能显著提升预测性能，说明专家选择轨迹中存在超出相邻层持续性的残差预测结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型将每个token路由经过一系列专家选择。一个自然假设是：最近一次（即上一层）的选择已充分概括整条轨迹，足以预测下一层路由器。作者质疑该假设是否成立，即历史层的选择是否携带额外、不可被最近状态替代的预测信息。

Method: 以最近一层选择为共同基线，测量更早层专家选择带来的留出预测增益。在冻结的OLMoE上将历史从1层扩展到11层；在JetMoE上进行预注册复现，取四层增益并报告成对bootstrap区间。采用线性探针、小型多层感知机等非线性解码器进行对照，设置参数匹配对照，并用交叉拟合的历史残差预测目标残差。

Result: OLMoE中，历史从1层扩展到11层使路由器logit的R²由0.59879升至0.66544。JetMoE预注册复现在两个目标深度上四层增益分别为0.14275和0.20528，成对bootstrap区间均高于零。增益在非线性解码下依然存在：向小型MLP加入历史使R²提升0.17137和0.21861，而仅对最近状态做非线性解码相对线性探针仅提升0.00139和0.00936。参数匹配对照保留该优势，交叉拟合历史残差对目标残差的预测R²为0.20549和0.23556。

Conclusion: 专家选择轨迹中存在超出相邻层持续性的残差预测结构；仅凭最近一次选择不足以充分概括路由历史。这一发现揭示MoE路由决策具有跨层依赖，对理解与潜在的改进路由预测和模型可解释性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts models route each token through a sequence of expert selections. We ask whether the immediately preceding selection adequately summarizes this trajectory for predicting the next router. Using frozen OLMoE and JetMoE models, we measure the held-out predictive gain from earlier expert selections while retaining the most recent selection as a common baseline. In OLMoE, extending the history from one to eleven layers raises router-logit $R^2$ from 0.59879 to 0.66544. A preregistered JetMoE replication yields four-layer gains of 0.14275 and 0.20528 at two target depths, with paired bootstrap intervals above zero. These gains survive nonlinear decoding: adding history to a small multilayer perceptron improves $R^2$ by 0.17137 and 0.21861, whereas nonlinear decoding of the recent state alone adds 0.00139 and 0.00936 over a linear probe. Parameter-matched controls preserve the advantage, and cross-fitted history residuals predict target residuals with $R^2$ of 0.20549 and 0.23556. These findings identify residual predictive structure in expert-selection trajectories beyond adjacent-layer persistence.

</details>


### [303] [On the Identifiability of Mixed Ordinal and Exponential Family Causal DAGs under Linear Parametric Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17942)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性参数模型（LPMs）中节点服从有序logit模型或一般单参数指数族时的可识别性问题，证明在有序节点至少三类别、指数族节点至少三支撑点的条件下，连接两类节点的每条边方向可由联合分布唯一识别，且条件是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结构方程模型及同质分布族节点的结果无法覆盖混合有序-指数族节点的情形，需确定这类模型边方向的辨识边界及所需最小条件。

Method: 针对节点服从有序logit或单参数指数族的线性参数模型，从联合分布出发分析边方向的可识别性，并通过构造反例推导条件必要性；在不同链接函数（仿射充分统计量、典范链接）下考察类别数与支撑点数的约束。

Result: 证明在有序节点≥3类别、指数族节点≥3支撑点且对充分统计量无限制时，每条混合边的方向在每个参数值下均可识别；反例表明三类别要求仅在仿射充分统计量时起约束作用，三支撑点要求仅在典范链接下起约束作用；该保证可推广至d节点无向骨架中所有此类混合边。

Conclusion: 混合有序-指数族边的方向可仅凭联合分布识别，数值实验成功在马尔可夫等价类内区分了仅靠条件独立性无法分辨的方向，验证了理论结果。

Abstract: The problem of identifiability in linear parametric models (LPMs) whose nodes follow either an ordered logit model or a regular one-parameter exponential family is evaluated. The results go beyond classical structural equation models as well as results for nodes with observations from a homogeneous family of distributions. The main result establishes that the orientation of every edge joining an ordinal node to an exponential-family node is identifiable from the joint distribution alone at every parameter value, provided the ordinal node has at least three categories and the exponential-family node at least three points of support, with no restriction on the sufficient statistic. Converses show that both requirements are necessary: the three-category requirement is binding only for affine sufficient statistics, and the three-point requirement is binding under the canonical link. The guarantee extends to orienting every such mixed ordinal-exponential family edge of a given $d$-node undirected skeleton. Numerical experiments illustrate the theoretical results by successfully separating orientations within a Markov equivalence class, which are indistinguishable by conditional independence alone.

</details>


### [304] [ASPIRE: Asynchronous Batched Self-Speculative Decoding for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17943)
*Amir Ziashahabi,Hossein Entezari Zarch,Lei Gao,Murali Annavaram,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASPIRE 提出一种非同步批处理自推测解码框架，通过混合前向、在线推测调度器和草稿内刷新层，解决了批内所有请求共享同一草稿-验证调度的同步瓶颈，在多个模型和基准上显著提升解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理受限于注意力机制：重复的 KV-cache 读取使解码成为内存受限。自推测解码通过稀疏注意力生成草稿、全注意力验证来缓解此问题，但现有批处理方法存在同步问题——批内所有请求共享单一草稿-验证调度，而最优草稿长度在不同请求间差异很大，且在同一请求内动态变化。

Method: ASPIRE 由三个组件构成：(1) 统一混合前向（unified mixed forward），允许草稿请求与验证请求共存于同一批前向传递中，消除全局草稿-验证阶段；(2) 轻量级在线推测调度器（online speculation scheduler），利用每请求接受率估计与批次感知成本模型，让每个请求独立决定何时验证；(3) 草稿内刷新层（intra-draft refresh layer），在草稿期间于单个指定层执行全注意力，在每个草稿步骤更新稀疏上下文，减少草稿过程中的陈旧性。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理及长上下文基准上，ASPIRE 相比自回归基线实现 1.70–4.58 倍的解码吞吐加速，并比最强的先验自推测基线平均加速约 27%。

Conclusion: ASPIRE 通过非同步批处理自推测解码框架，有效打破批内同步调度限制，让各请求独立自适应选择草稿与验证时机，从而在长上下文推理中实现显著的解码吞吐提升。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by attention, whose repeated KV-cache reads make decoding memory-bound. Self-speculative decoding alleviates this by drafting tokens with sparse attention and verifying them with full attention, but existing batched methods remain synchronized: all requests in a batch share a single draft-verify schedule, even though the optimal draft length varies widely across requests and changes dynamically within each request. We propose ASPIRE, a non-synchronized batched self-speculative decoding framework built on three components. First, a unified mixed forward allows drafting and verifying requests to coexist in the same batched forward pass, removing the need for global draft-verify phases. Second, a lightweight online speculation scheduler uses per-request acceptance-rate estimates and a batch-aware cost model to let each request independently choose when to verify. Third, an intra-draft refresh layer performs full attention at a single designated layer during drafting, updating the sparse context at every draft step to reduce staleness during drafting. Across three models and five reasoning and long-context benchmarks, ASPIRE achieves $1.70$-$4.58\times$ speedup in decoding throughput over autoregressive baselines and improves average speedup by approximately $27\%$ over the strongest prior self-speculative baselines.

</details>


### [305] [Maximum Strong Independent Sets in Hypergraphs: Reductions, Bounds, and Greedy Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17951)
*Yingquan,Wu,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有限超图中的最大强独立集问题（选取使每条超边最多含一个顶点的最大顶点集），建立关联结构工具包，给出精确归约、上界、证书及贪婪算法分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该目标源于每块观测为局部不相容约束、而跨重叠块做传递闭包不合理的情形。典型动机是多频段 LSH-MinHash 去重：冲突桶提供局部证据，连通分量收缩会引入伪全局等价。

Method: 提出关联结构工具包：证明支配、关联孪生、权重为1块的精确归约；推导闭式与低权重上界；引入穿刺与覆盖证书以收紧上界；分析由块权重和剩余关联驱动的一层贪婪聚类算法。

Result: 算法分析涵盖可行性、极大性、条件最优性、一层见证匹配上界及关联局部复杂度界；为广泛关联族给出正确性、终止、不动点与最优性证书，并给出不同证书分离或重合的示例。

Conclusion: 该工作为最大强独立集提供系统的关联结构理论与多类证书，揭示证书间的分离与重合条件，并适用于以局部约束为核心的应用场景。

Abstract: We study the maximum strong independent set problem in a finite hypergraph: find the largest vertex set that intersects every hyperedge in at most one vertex. This objective arises whenever each observed block is a local incompatibility constraint but transitive closure across overlapping blocks is not justified. A motivating example is multi-band LSH-MinHash deduplication, where each collision bucket gives local evidence, while connected-component contraction can impose spurious global equivalences. The paper develops an incidence-structural toolkit for this problem. We prove exact reductions for dominance, incidence twins, and weight-1 blocks; derive closed-form and low-weight upper bounds; introduce puncturing and covering certificates that sharpen those bounds; and analyze a layered greedy clustering algorithm driven by block weights and residual incidence. The algorithmic analysis includes feasibility, maximality, conditional optimality, a layered witness-matching upper bound, and incidence-local complexity bounds. The results give correctness, termination, fixed-point, and optimality certificates for broad incidence families, together with examples showing when different certificates separate or coincide.

</details>


### [306] [QuanText: Protecting Dataset-Level Secrets in Textual Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17995)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuanText——一种无需训练、与具体大语言模型无关的文本数据发布机制，通过随机量化扰动候选发布分布，在保护数据集级全局秘密（如某类诊断的比例）的同时保留主题、情感等属性的数据效用，并在真实数据集上取得优于对照生成基线的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据集虽支撑大量下游应用与研究，但文本发布可能泄露数据源的全局敏感属性（如性别、诊断、政治立场的记录占比）。现有研究多聚焦于上述全局属性的属性推断攻击，而针对数据集级秘密的防护手段有限；差分隐私虽擅长保护个体记录，却对聚合属性的保护较弱。因此需要一个能在文本上保护全局秘密且尽量保持数据效用的发布机制。

Method: 提出 Randomized Quantization for Text（QuanText）：一种训练-free、LLM-agnostic 的数据发布机制。输入为数据集级秘密（例如某诊断的记录占比）与需保留效用的属性（如主题与情感）。方法在秘密属性与相关非秘密属性的维度上构造候选发布分布，随机选择一个与私有经验分布足够接近的候选分布，再利用原文中与属性相关的片段将每条私有文本样本重写以匹配所选分布。机制受 Statistic Maximal Leakage（SML）框架启发，SML 用于界定关于数据分布的某个秘密函数的泄露上界。

Result: 在理想化条件下，文中证明 QuanText 满足 SML 保证；考虑到这些条件在实践中未必严格成立，作者在真实数据集上进行了经验评估，结果显示 QuanText 在隐私-效用权衡方面优于与之竞争的生成式基线方法。

Conclusion: QuanText 为文本数据集中的全局秘密提供了一种无需训练、模型无关的防护方案，兼顾数据效用；在理想条件下具备 SML 理论保证，并在实证中展现出更优的隐私-效用权衡，表明其可作为现有数据生成式防护方法的有力替代。

Abstract: Natural-language datasets support many downstream applications and research studies, but releasing text can reveal sensitive global properties of the underlying data source, such as the proportion of records associated with a particular gender, diagnosis, or political stance. Existing work has largely focused on property inference attacks that recover such global properties, while defenses for protecting these dataset-level secrets remain limited. Differential privacy, although effective for protecting individual records, provides only weak protection for aggregate properties. We propose Randomized Quantization for Text (QuanText), a training-free and large-language-model-agnostic data release mechanism that protects global secrets in textual datasets while preserving data utility. Given a dataset-level secret, such as the proportion of records with a particular diagnosis, and attributes whose utility should be preserved, such as topic and sentiment, QuanText perturbs both the secret distribution and the distributions of correlated attributes. It does so by constructing candidate release distributions over secret and non-secret attributes, randomly selecting a candidate sufficiently close to the private empirical distribution, and rewriting each private text sample to match the selected distribution using attribute-related snippets from the original text. QuanText is inspired by the Statistic Maximal Leakage (SML) framework, which bounds leakage about a secret function of a data distribution. Under idealized conditions, we show that QuanText satisfies an SML guarantee. Since these conditions may not hold exactly in practice, we also evaluate QuanText empirically on real-world datasets. Our results show that QuanText achieves a better empirical privacy-utility trade-off than competing data generation baselines.

</details>


### [307] [The Attention Within: Consensus Dynamics in Selective State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17997)
*João Pedro Silvestre,Álvaro Rodríguez Abella,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究选择性状态空间模型（SSM）中递归的token混合机制，将其跨层演化建模为常微分方程，利用输入-状态稳定性（ISS）证明共识平衡点的局部指数稳定性及其吸引域，揭示SSM核心递归与Transformer注意力一样驱动token趋向共识，并在Mamba-2上验证输出门是调节该共识程度的关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性SSM已成为Transformer的有力替代方案，在保持竞争力性能的同时显著提升推理效率。已有工作表明SSM的递归可写成类似线性注意力的形式，二者架构比表面看起来更接近。在Transformer中，注意力已知会驱动token聚簇、达成共识并最终坍缩到单一方向。由此产生核心疑问：SSM核心的递归是否也像注意力一样驱动token走向共识？

Method: 作者采用动力系统视角，将token跨层的演化建模为常微分方程（ODE）。通过运用输入-状态稳定性（ISS）论证，针对时变权重矩阵（此前结果未涉及的设定）建立共识平衡点的局部指数稳定性，并刻画其吸引域。

Result: 理论分析证明SSM与Transformer的相似性更为深刻：SSM核心递归确实像注意力一样聚合token。在预训练Mamba-2模型上的数值实验表明，输出门是调节这一共识程度的组件，它阻止token完全达成共识。

Conclusion: SSM核心递归与Transformer注意力在驱动token共识方面具有本质相似性。输出门作为调节器控制共识的程度，使token不至于完全坍缩。这为理解SSM的信息混合机制提供了动力系统层面的理论框架。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to transformers, combining competitive performance with substantially improved inference efficiency. At each SSM layer, a sequence of hidden states are propagated by a recurrence, mixing information of different tokens. Despite using a different mechanism, this mixing plays a role analogous to attention in transformers. In fact, recent works have shown that the two architectures may be closer than they first appear, as this recurrence admits a formulation akin to linear attention. In transformers, attention is known to drive the tokens to cluster, i.e., to reach consensus, collapsing in the limit to a single direction. Thus, we ask: does the recurrence at the core of SSMs drive the tokens to consensus, as attention does in transformers?
  To answer this question, we take a dynamical systems perspective on SSMs, modeling the evolution of tokens across layers as an ordinary differential equation. By exploiting input-to-state stability arguments, we establish local exponential stability of the consensus equilibria and characterize their domain of attraction for time-varying weight matrices, a setting not addressed by previous results. We thereby show that the resemblance between SSMs and transformers does run deeper: the recurrence at the core of SSMs aggregates tokens just as attention does. Numerical experiments on a pretrained Mamba-2 model point to the output gate as the component that regulates the extent of this consensus, preventing the tokens from reaching it in full.

</details>


### [308] [Structural Inference under Hidden Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18045)
*Zhongben Gong,Xiaoqun Wu,Mingyang Zhou,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"隐藏智能体下的结构推断（SIHA）"问题，并设计"结构无关初始化+结构引导迭代精化"框架，联合恢复隐藏智能体轨迹及其交互结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多智能体动力学中恢复潜在交互结构对理解与预测交互系统很重要。现有基于轨迹的结构推断假设所有被建模智能体的轨迹均可用，但部署时因感知受限、遮挡或通信故障，智能体可能不可观测。已有工作分别研究未知节点估计、部分观测下的结构推断与缺失值填补，但隐藏智能体轨迹与其交互的联合恢复仍未被充分探索。

Method: 将该问题形式化为"隐藏智能体下的结构推断"，其核心难点是循环依赖：恢复涉及隐藏智能体的交互需要其轨迹估计，而轨迹重建又受益于结构信息。为此提出SIHA，结合结构无关初始化与结构引导的迭代精化：从可见观测重建隐藏轨迹，用神经关系推断（NRI）推断交互，并通过多强度结构注意力与迭代的状态—结构更新将估计结构反馈至隐藏状态重建。

Result: 在三个基准动力学系统上实验，可见—可见结构推断取得一致提升，同时在隐藏状态重建与未来预测上也有收益。在模拟整肢遮挡的动作捕捉实验中进一步验证了其在真实隐藏智能体场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: SIHA通过耦合轨迹重建与结构推断，有效缓解循环依赖，实现隐藏智能体轨迹与交互结构的联合恢复，在结构推断、状态重建与预测任务上均表现更优，并具备真实场景适用性。

Abstract: Recovering latent interaction structures from multi-agent dynamics is important for understanding and predicting interacting systems. Trajectory-based structural inference has achieved promising performance, but conventional formulations assume that the trajectories of all modeled agents are available. In practice, agents may become unobserved at deployment because of limited sensing, occlusion, or communication failure. Existing studies have considered unseen-node estimation, structural inference under partial observations, and missing-value imputation, yet the joint recovery of hidden-agent trajectories and their interactions remains underexplored. We formulate this problem as structural inference under hidden agents. Its key difficulty is a circular dependency: recovering interactions involving a hidden agent requires an estimate of its trajectory, while trajectory reconstruction can itself benefit from structural information. To address this challenge, we propose Structural Inference under Hidden Agents (SIHA), which combines structure-agnostic initialization with structure-guided iterative refinement. SIHA reconstructs hidden trajectories from visible observations, infers interactions using Neural Relational Inference, and feeds the estimated structure back into hidden-state reconstruction through multi-strength structural attention and iterative state--structure updates. Experiments on three benchmark dynamical systems demonstrate consistent improvements in visible-to-visible structural inference, while also showing benefits in hidden-state reconstruction and future prediction. Motion-capture experiments with simulated whole-limb occlusion further demonstrate its effectiveness in realistic hidden-agent settings.

</details>


### [309] [Regional Explanations via Causal Sufficiency and Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18049)
*Xuexin Chen,Peng Liang,Zijian Li,Zhiyong Lin,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SNRE框架，通过学习的输入区域A与输出区域B，使A的成员资格成为模型输出落入B的充分必要条件，基于PNS度量与可微有限样本估计进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法多以特征重要性、反事实解释或规则形式解释预测，但对"何时且仅在何时"某预测行为出现的区域级刻画研究不足。

Method: 受概率必要性充分性(PNS)启发，通过随机干预定义区域级PNS度量，推导可微有限样本估计器用于优化；用显式可解释的代数区域族加可学习特征掩码来参数化输入-输出区域对，兼顾表达力与可解释性。

Result: 实验表明SNRE学到的区域对具备强的充分性-必要性性能、稳健的解释行为，并在模型分析中具有实际效用。

Conclusion: SNRE为模型预测行为提供区域级的充分必要解释，是区域级可解释性的一种有效且实用的新框架。

Abstract: Model explainability is essential for understanding and trusting machine learning models. Existing explainable AI methods often explain predictions through feature importance, counterfactual explanations, or rules. However, a region-level characterization of when and only when a prediction behavior arises remains less explored. This paper proposes Causal Sufficient and Necessary Regional Explanations (SNRE), a framework that learns an input region $A$ and output region $B$ such that membership in $A$ is both sufficient and necessary for the model output to fall in $B$. Motivated by the classical Probability of Necessity and Sufficiency (PNS), we formulate a region-level PNS measure through stochastic interventions and derive a differentiable finite-sample estimator for optimization. SNRE parameterizes the input-output region pair with explicit and interpretable algebraic region families, together with a learnable feature mask, balancing expressiveness and interpretability. Experiments demonstrate that SNRE learns region pairs with strong sufficiency-necessity performance, robust explanation behavior, and practical utility for model analysis.

</details>


### [310] [Beyond Embedding Transfer: Component Roles in Grokking Transfer and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18078)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在模算术的跨算子迁移任务中，将迁移收益拆分为"早期加速(efficacy)"与"到达后稳定性(stability)"，通过大规模因子实验与前瞻性复现，明确内部注意力/MLP块的贡献、揭示embedding/readout的作用边界，并提出冻结表示载体与在线门控两种抑制"grokking后回退"的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: warm-start迁移能让算法任务快速泛化，但此前不清楚究竟是哪些模型组件带来收益，也不清楚该收益在持续优化下是否稳定。作者希望把迁移效应解耦为早期速度与轨迹稳定性两个维度，并厘清参数"屏蔽/冻结"策略的有效边界。

Method: 在模算术任务上做跨算子迁移，设计规模匹配的108次运行（12个种子块，含96次2^3因子实验加12次规模对照）。对比仅迁移token embedding与readout(E+U)与额外迁移注意力/MLP内部权重(B)的配置；用Holm校正、TOST等价检验评估延迟；做2层模型的前瞻性复现；并用冻结表示载体(E,U)与在线验证触发的门控(online gating)抑制回退，最后在非阿贝尔S_5上检验屏蔽策略。

Result: 迁移内部注意力/MLP权重(B)使早期准确率提升5.46个百分点(Holm p=0.0039)，确认延迟减少558步(Holm p=0.0088)。1层模型中readout加内部块迁移满足±500步等价标准(TOST p=0.0011)，但Full在11/12配对种子中更快；2层复现确认内部块优势(12/12种子，+704.67积分单位，p=4.88×10^-4)，并暴露架构边界——去掉供体embedding后比Full低4475.6单位，超出±250单位容差。冻结迁移的表示载体(E,U)几乎消除离线回退(19.40%→0.07%，Holm p=0.005859)；在线门控把True Max Drawdown从22.06%降到0.60%(p=0.000488)，并推广到affine、非线性二次及2层目标(降低10.94–23.47 pp)。在非阿贝尔S_5上无屏蔽迁移出现短暂峰值(95.4%)，但前瞻性屏蔽组无确认收益(+0.15±1.14 pp)。

Conclusion: 结果建立了迁移加速与轨迹稳定性之间的组件级解耦：内部块驱动早期加速，冻结表示载体与在线门控负责持续稳定，而参数屏蔽存在经验边界（在S_5等情形失效）。这提示迁移加速与稳定性可由不同组件分别调控，而非单一机制。

Abstract: Warm-start transfer can make algorithmic tasks generalize rapidly, yet it is unclear which model components provide the gain and whether that gain remains stable under continued optimization. We study cross-operator transfer on modular arithmetic and separate efficacy (early velocity) from stability (post-reach drawdown). In a scale-matched 108-run battery across 12 seed blocks (96-run 2^3 factorial plus 12-run scale control), transferring internal attention/MLP weights (B) alongside token embeddings and readout (E+U) improves early accuracy by 5.46 pp (Holm p=0.0039) and cuts confirmation latency by 558 steps (Holm p=0.0088).
